 void Bind(Material m){var f=eye.full;var camera=f.motion.camera;m.SetMatrix("_ProbeGPUVP91",GL.GetGPUProjectionMatrix(OutputSupport26.Crop(ROI,f.effectiveProjection),true)*camera.worldToCameraMatrix);m.SetFloat("_RasterScale91",4);m.SetVector("_ROI348",new Vector4(ROI.x,272-ROI.y-ROI.height,ROI.width,ROI.height));m.SetVector("_SurfaceInvRaster335",new Vector4(1f/480,1f/272,0,0));m.SetFloat("_FaceShadeSteps335",f.faceShadeSteps);m.SetFloat("_SurfaceShadeSteps335",f.surfaceShadeSteps);m.SetInt("_SurfaceMaskReady335",0);m.SetFloat("_BindSH91",0);}
 public override void RecordRenderGraph(RenderGraph graph,ContextContainer frame){if(!Enabled154||Disposed||frame.Get<UniversalCameraData>().camera!=eye.full.motion.camera)return;ROI=eye.roi;var r=frame.Get<UniversalResourceData>();var ld155=frame.Get<UniversalLightData>();var lights155=new Arr40();for(int n155=0;n155<Math.Min(16,ld155.visibleLights.Length);n155++){var l155=ld155.visibleLights[n155];lights155.Add(new Obj40{["index"]=n155,["type"]=l155.lightType.ToString(),["matrix"]=new Arr40(Enumerable.Range(0,16).Select(k=>(double)l155.localToWorldMatrix[k])),["colour"]=new Arr40(l155.finalColor.r,l155.finalColor.g,l155.finalColor.b,l155.finalColor.a),["range"]=l155.range,["source"]=l155.light?l155.light.GetEntityId().ToString():"none"});}Light155=new Obj40{["frame"]=Time.frameCount,["time"]=Time.timeAsDouble,["mainIndex"]=ld155.mainLightIndex,["additionalCount"]=ld155.additionalLightsCount,["visibleCount"]=ld155.visibleLights.Length,["lights"]=lights155,["truncated"]=ld155.visibleLights.Length>16};BeforeCanvas?.Invoke(graph,r);Targets();foreach(var m in mats)Bind(m);using(var b=graph.AddUnsafePass<Data>("101 clean native face and skin socket proxy",out var d)){d.owner=this;foreach(var t in fields)b.UseTexture(graph.ImportTexture(t),AccessFlags.Write);b.UseTexture(graph.ImportTexture(depth),AccessFlags.Write);b.UseAllGlobalTextures(true);if(r.mainShadowsTexture.IsValid())b.UseTexture(r.mainShadowsTexture);if(r.additionalShadowsTexture.IsValid())b.UseTexture(r.additionalShadowsTexture);b.AllowPassCulling(false);b.AllowGlobalStateModification(true);b.SetRenderFunc(static(Data data,UnsafeGraphContext context)=>data.owner.Draw(context));}AfterCanvas?.Invoke(graph,r);}
 void Draw(UnsafeGraphContext context){var cmd=CommandBufferHelpers.GetNativeCommandBuffer(context.cmd);cmd.SetRenderTarget(fields.Select(t=>new RenderTargetIdentifier(t.rt)).ToArray(),depth.rt);cmd.ClearRenderTarget(RTClearFlags.All,Color.clear,1,0);cmd.SetViewport(new Rect(0,0,width,height));var matrix=eye.full.motion.surface.outputs[1].transform.localToWorldMatrix;
  cmd.DrawMesh(eye.sampleFace,matrix,skin,3,0);cmd.DrawMesh(eye.sampleFace,matrix,white,4,0);cmd.DrawMesh(eye.sampleFace,matrix,iris,1,0);RenderedFrame=Time.frameCount;
 }
 public void AssertRendered(){if(failure!=null)throw new InvalidOperationException("101 pass enqueue failed",failure);if(RenderedFrame!=Time.frameCount)throw new InvalidOperationException("101 no same-frame in-graph canvas; no manual fallback");}
 void Release(){if(fields!=null)foreach(var t in fields)t?.Release();depth?.Release();fields=null;depth=null;}
 public void Dispose(){if(Disposed)return;Disposed=true;RenderPipelineManager.beginCameraRendering-=Enqueue;BeforeCanvas=null;AfterCanvas=null;Release();if(mats!=null)foreach(var m in mats)if(m)UnityEngine.Object.DestroyImmediate(m);}
}


// Explicit runtime view/owned materials/owned RTs. Never writes globals, borrowed
// source geometry, original shaders/materials, InputSettings or product output.
public sealed class NativeRetarget96:IDisposable{
 public const string SurfaceAsset="Assets/EUnityValidation01/EyeBoundaryRestore348/Shaders/NativeSurface89.shader";
 public const string SkinAsset="Assets/EUnityValidation01/EyeBoundaryRestore348/Shaders/NativeSkin89.shader";
 readonly bool diagnostics;readonly EyeFeature348 eye;readonly UnityEngine.Mesh sourceMesh;readonly Material[] materials=new Material[8];
 readonly int[] parts={3,1,4,6};readonly Dictionary<int,Vector4[]> projection=new Dictionary<int,Vector4[]>();
 readonly Dictionary<int,Vector2> anchors=new Dictionary<int,Vector2>();
 public RenderTexture[][] layers;public RenderTexture[] depths;public Obj58 Info{get;private set;}
 public bool Disposed{get;private set;}public int currentWidth,currentHeight;int controlConvention=-1;float screenSign=-1;
 // Json58 has int and double conversion operators but no float operator.
 // A direct (float)Json58 selects its int conversion and rounds the source.
 static float F(Json58 number)=>(float)(double)number;
 public NativeRetarget96(EyeFeature348 e,string registrationFile,string diagnosticFile,bool fullDiagnostics=false){diagnostics=fullDiagnostics;
  eye=e;sourceMesh=e.sampleFace;if(!eye||!eye.full||!sourceMesh||EditorUtility.IsPersistent(eye))throw new InvalidOperationException("Live prepared source view required");
  var reg=Json58.Parse(File.ReadAllText(registrationFile));
  foreach(var row in reg["eyes"]){int owner=(int)row["eye"];var P=row["nativeToAuthoredProjection"];projection[owner]=new[]{new Vector4(F(P[0][0]),F(P[1][0]),F(P[2][0]),F(P[3][0])),new Vector4(F(P[0][1]),F(P[1][1]),F(P[2][1]),F(P[3][1]))};var cells=reg["cells"].Where(c=>(int)c["eye"]==owner).ToArray();float minX=cells.Min(c=>F(c["sourceXY"][0])),maxX=cells.Max(c=>F(c["sourceXY"][0])),minY=cells.Min(c=>F(c["sourceXY"][1])),maxY=cells.Max(c=>F(c["sourceXY"][1]));anchors[owner]=new Vector2(Mathf.Floor((minX+maxX)/2)+.5f,Mathf.Floor((minY+maxY)/2)+.5f);}
  var rest=new List<Vector3>();sourceMesh.GetUVs(1,rest);float error=0;var measured=new Arr58();
  foreach(var row in reg["eyes"]){int owner=(int)row["eye"];var ids=row["nativeRingVertexIds"];for(int i=0;i<ids.Count;i++){var v=rest[(int)ids[i]];var q=new Vector4(v.x,v.y,v.z,1);var target=row["authoredParent"][i];var actual=new Vector2(Vector4.Dot(q,projection[owner][0]),Vector4.Dot(q,projection[owner][1]));error=Mathf.Max(error,Vector2.Distance(actual,new Vector2(F(target[0]),F(target[1]))));measured.Add(new Obj58{["owner"]=owner,["ringIndex"]=i,["vertex"]=(int)ids[i],["rest"]=new Arr58(v.x,v.y,v.z),["projected"]=new Arr58(actual.x,actual.y),["target"]=target});}}
  CreateOnly40.Text(diagnosticFile,new Obj58{["maxError"]=error,["headBoneIndex"]=eye.headBoneIndex,["sourceTopology"]=eye.modelTopologySHA256,["sourceMesh"]=sourceMesh.name,["sourceMeshId"]=sourceMesh.GetEntityId().ToString(),["uv1Count"]=rest.Count,["sourceToRestHead"]=new Arr58(Enumerable.Range(0,16).Select(i=>(double)eye.sourceToRestHead[i])),["projection"]=new Arr58(projection.Select(p=>new Obj58{["owner"]=p.Key,["x"]=new Arr58(p.Value[0].x,p.Value[0].y,p.Value[0].z,p.Value[0].w),["y"]=new Arr58(p.Value[1].x,p.Value[1].y,p.Value[1].z,p.Value[1].w)})),["measurements"]=measured}.ToString());
  if(error>1e-3)throw new InvalidOperationException("Current source rest/boundary registration mismatch "+error);
  try{for(int i=0;i<8;i++){int role=parts[i/2];var src=role==3?eye.skinMaterial:eye.materials[4+role];var shader=AssetDatabase.LoadAssetAtPath<Shader>(role==3?SkinAsset:SurfaceAsset);if(!src||!shader||!shader.isSupported)throw new InvalidOperationException("Retarget source shader/material unavailable");var keywords=src.shaderKeywords;materials[i]=new Material(src){shader=shader,hideFlags=HideFlags.DontSave,name="95 owned native source part "+role+" eye-slot "+(i%2)};materials[i].shaderKeywords=keywords;}}
  catch{Dispose();throw;}
  Info=new Obj58{["registrationMaxPixels"]=error,["sourceMeshId"]=sourceMesh.GetEntityId().ToString(),["borrowedMeshMutated"]=false,["globalWrites"]=0,["productBindingsChanged"]=false};
 }
 RenderTexture Colour(int w,int h,string name){var d=new RenderTextureDescriptor(w,h){graphicsFormat=GraphicsFormat.R32G32B32A32_SFloat,depthStencilFormat=GraphicsFormat.None,msaaSamples=1,sRGB=false};var t=new RenderTexture(d){filterMode=FilterMode.Point,hideFlags=HideFlags.DontSave,name=name};if(!t.Create()){UnityEngine.Object.DestroyImmediate(t);throw new InvalidOperationException("Own colour RT creation failed");}return t;}
 RenderTexture Depth(int w,int h){var d=new RenderTextureDescriptor(w,h){graphicsFormat=GraphicsFormat.None,depthStencilFormat=GraphicsFormat.D32_SFloat,msaaSamples=1};var t=new RenderTexture(d){filterMode=FilterMode.Point,hideFlags=HideFlags.DontSave,name="89 owned depth"};if(!t.Create()){UnityEngine.Object.DestroyImmediate(t);throw new InvalidOperationException("Own depth RT creation failed");}return t;}
 void ReleaseTargets(){ReleaseWrappers();if(layers!=null)foreach(var a in layers)if(a!=null)foreach(var t in a)if(t){t.Release();UnityEngine.Object.DestroyImmediate(t);}if(depths!=null)foreach(var t in depths)if(t){t.Release();UnityEngine.Object.DestroyImmediate(t);}layers=null;depths=null;}
 void Targets(int w,int h){if(layers!=null&&w==currentWidth&&h==currentHeight)return;ReleaseTargets();currentWidth=w;currentHeight=h;layers=new RenderTexture[4][];depths=new RenderTexture[4];try{for(int i=0;i<4;i++){layers[i]=new RenderTexture[5];for(int ch=0;ch<5;ch++)layers[i][ch]=Colour(w,h,"89 source "+parts[i]+" channel "+ch);depths[i]=Depth(w,h);}}catch{ReleaseTargets();throw;}}
 Matrix4x4 Clip(bool gpu){var cam=eye.full.motion.camera;return(gpu?GL.GetGPUProjectionMatrix(eye.cropProjection,true):eye.cropProjection)*cam.worldToCameraMatrix;}
 // Homography335 stores a 3x3 projective transform in a Matrix4x4 whose
 // fourth row/column are zero. Embed the independent homogeneous dimension
 // before inversion; Matrix4x4.inverse on the original singular 4x4 is invalid.
 static Matrix4x4 Forward(Matrix4x4 inverse){inverse.m33=1;if(Mathf.Abs(inverse.determinant)<1e-12f)throw new InvalidOperationException("Native source homography singular");return inverse.inverse;}
 void Bind(Material mat,int layer,int owner,Vector2 anchor,Matrix4x4 canonical,int control,bool gpu){
  var f=eye.full;var cam=f.motion.camera;mat.SetInt("_Control89",control);mat.SetInt("_UseBank89",control==0?1:0);mat.SetInt("_SelectedOwner89",owner);mat.SetMatrix("_PhysicalCropGPUVP89",Clip(gpu));mat.SetMatrix("_PhysicalView89",cam.worldToCameraMatrix);mat.SetMatrix("_WorldToHead348",eye.headMatrix.inverse);mat.SetVector("_PhysicalCamera89",new Vector4(cam.transform.position.x,cam.transform.position.y,cam.transform.position.z,cam.orthographic?1:0));mat.SetVector("_PhysicalForward89",cam.transform.forward);mat.SetVector("_SourcePhysicalROI89",new Vector4(eye.roi.x,272-eye.roi.y-eye.roi.height,eye.roi.width,eye.roi.height));mat.SetVector("_SourcePhysicalSize89",new Vector4(eye.roi.width*4,eye.roi.height*4,0,0));mat.SetVector("_CanonicalAnchor89",anchor);mat.SetMatrix("_CanonicalGPUProjection89",canonical);mat.SetInt("_SupportKind348",layer);mat.SetVector("_Profile333",f.motion.animation.shadeProfile);mat.SetVector("_SurfaceInvRaster335",new Vector4(1f/480,1f/272,0,0));mat.SetFloat("_SurfaceOwnShade335",f.motion.animation.shadeProfile.x);mat.SetFloat("_ShadeLayer335",f.shadeLayer?1:0);mat.SetFloat("_SurfaceShadeSteps335",f.surfaceShadeSteps);mat.SetTexture("_FeatureBankRGB89",f.templateRGBA);mat.SetTexture("_FeatureBankRole89",f.templateRole);
  mat.SetInt("_SupportKind348",layer==1?2:layer==2?1:layer);if(owner>0){int i=Array.FindIndex(eye.centres,c=>Mathf.RoundToInt(c.w)==owner);mat.SetVector("_NativeSourceX89",projection[owner][0]);mat.SetVector("_NativeSourceY89",projection[owner][1]);mat.SetMatrix("_AuthoredToHead89",Forward(f.eyeInverse[i]));}
 }

 public RTHandle[][] Handles96=>wrappers;
 public Vector2 Anchor96{get;private set;}public Vector4 Bounds96{get;private set;}
 RTHandle[][] wrappers;RTHandle[] depthWrappers;RTHandle[] controlHandles;RTHandle controlDepth;Material controlMaterial;
 public RenderTexture[] controlFields;public int RenderedFrame{get;private set;}=-1;public int ControlFrame{get;private set;}=-1;
 sealed class Data{public NativeRetarget96 owner;public int part;}
 void ReleaseWrappers(){if(wrappers!=null)foreach(var a in wrappers)foreach(var x in a)x?.Release();if(depthWrappers!=null)foreach(var x in depthWrappers)x?.Release();wrappers=null;depthWrappers=null;}
 void Wrap(){if(wrappers!=null)return;wrappers=layers.Select(a=>a.Select(t=>RTHandles.Alloc(t,transferOwnership:false)).ToArray()).ToArray();depthWrappers=depths.Select(t=>RTHandles.Alloc(t,transferOwnership:false)).ToArray();}
 void ControlTargets(){int w=eye.roi.width*4,h=eye.roi.height*4;if(controlHandles!=null&&controlFields[0].width==w&&controlFields[0].height==h)return;if(controlHandles!=null)foreach(var t in controlHandles)t?.Release();controlDepth?.Release();controlHandles=new RTHandle[5];controlFields=new RenderTexture[5];for(int i=0;i<5;i++){controlHandles[i]=RTHandles.Alloc(w,h,colorFormat:GraphicsFormat.R32G32B32A32_SFloat,filterMode:FilterMode.Point,name:"95 source projected-coordinate control");controlFields[i]=controlHandles[i].rt;}controlDepth=RTHandles.Alloc(w,h,depthBufferBits:DepthBits.Depth32);if(!controlMaterial){var src=eye.materials[10];controlMaterial=new Material(src){shader=AssetDatabase.LoadAssetAtPath<Shader>(SurfaceAsset),hideFlags=HideFlags.DontSave};controlMaterial.shaderKeywords=src.shaderKeywords;}}
 public void RecordGraph(RenderGraph graph,UniversalResourceData resources){
  if(Disposed||!eye||eye.sampleFace!=sourceMesh)throw new InvalidOperationException("95 source/view lifetime changed");
  var f=eye.full;var current=new Vector2[2];var all=new List<Vector2>();for(int i=0;i<2;i++){int owner=Mathf.RoundToInt(eye.centres[i].w);var h=Forward(f.eyeInverse[i]);Vector2 Project(Vector2 p){var q=h*new Vector4(p.x,p.y,1,0);return new Vector2(q.x/q.z,q.y/q.z);}current[i]=Project(anchors[owner]);foreach(var p in f.contours.eyes[i].parent)all.Add(Project(p));}
  var anchor=(current[0]+current[1])*.5f;var min=new Vector2(Mathf.Floor(all.Min(p=>p.x)-anchor.x)-2,Mathf.Floor(all.Min(p=>p.y)-anchor.y)-2);var max=new Vector2(Mathf.Ceil(all.Max(p=>p.x)-anchor.x)+2,Mathf.Ceil(all.Max(p=>p.y)-anchor.y)+2);int w=Mathf.RoundToInt((max.x-min.x)*4),height=Mathf.RoundToInt((max.y-min.y)*4);if(w*height>32768)throw new InvalidOperationException("95 viewport budget");Targets(w,height);Wrap();if(diagnostics)ControlTargets();var cam=f.motion.camera;var canonical=GL.GetGPUProjectionMatrix(Matrix4x4.Ortho(min.x,max.x,-max.y,-min.y,cam.nearClipPlane,cam.farClipPlane),true);
  Anchor96=anchor;Bounds96=new Vector4(min.x,min.y,max.x,max.y);screenSign=-1;if(diagnostics){Bind(controlMaterial,3,0,Vector2.zero,Matrix4x4.identity,1,true);controlMaterial.SetFloat("_ScreenSign89",screenSign);}
  for(int part=0;part<4;part++)for(int i=0;i<2;i++){var mat=materials[part*2+i];Bind(mat,part,Mathf.RoundToInt(eye.centres[i].w),anchor,canonical,0,true);mat.SetFloat("_ScreenSign89",screenSign);}
  for(int group=diagnostics?4:3;group>=0;group--){using var builder=graph.AddUnsafePass<Data>("95 source-local coupled parts with real graph shadows "+group,out var data);data.owner=this;data.part=group;var colours=group==4?controlHandles:wrappers[group];var depth=group==4?controlDepth:depthWrappers[group];foreach(var t in colours)builder.UseTexture(graph.ImportTexture(t),AccessFlags.Write);builder.UseTexture(graph.ImportTexture(depth),AccessFlags.Write);builder.UseAllGlobalTextures(true);if(resources.mainShadowsTexture.IsValid())builder.UseTexture(resources.mainShadowsTexture);if(resources.additionalShadowsTexture.IsValid())builder.UseTexture(resources.additionalShadowsTexture);builder.AllowPassCulling(false);builder.AllowGlobalStateModification(true);builder.SetRenderFunc(static(Data d,UnsafeGraphContext c)=>d.owner.DrawGraph(d.part,c));}
  Arr58 M(Matrix4x4 m)=>new Arr58(Enumerable.Range(0,16).Select(i=>(double)m[i]));Info["anchor"]=new Arr58(anchor.x,anchor.y);Info["bounds"]=new Arr58(min.x,min.y,max.x,max.y);Info["size"]=new Arr58(w,height);Info["canonicalGPUProjection"]=M(canonical);Info["physicalView"]=M(cam.worldToCameraMatrix);Info["headMatrix"]=M(eye.headMatrix);Info["parts"]=new Arr58(parts);Info["sampler"]=4;Info["sourceFrame"]=f.motion.sourceFrame;Info["independentMaterialsPerPartAndEye"]=true;Info["graphContext"]=true;Info["borrowedTextureOwnershipTransferred"]=false;
 }
 void DrawGraph(int part,UnsafeGraphContext context){var cmd=CommandBufferHelpers.GetNativeCommandBuffer(context.cmd);bool control=part==4;var textures=control?controlFields:layers[part];cmd.SetRenderTarget(textures.Select(t=>new RenderTargetIdentifier(t)).ToArray(),control?controlDepth.rt:depths[part]);cmd.ClearRenderTarget(RTClearFlags.All,Color.clear,1,0);cmd.SetViewport(new Rect(0,0,textures[0].width,textures[0].height));var model=eye.full.motion.surface.outputs[1].transform.localToWorldMatrix;if(control){cmd.DrawMesh(sourceMesh,model,controlMaterial,6,0);ControlFrame=Time.frameCount;}else{for(int i=0;i<2;i++)cmd.DrawMesh(sourceMesh,model,materials[part*2+i],parts[part],0);if(part==0)RenderedFrame=Time.frameCount;}}
 public void AssertRendered(){if(RenderedFrame!=Time.frameCount||(diagnostics&&ControlFrame!=Time.frameCount))throw new InvalidOperationException("95 source graph pass missing; no fallback draw");}
 public void Dispose(){if(Disposed)return;Disposed=true;ReleaseTargets();if(controlHandles!=null)foreach(var t in controlHandles)t?.Release();controlDepth?.Release();if(controlMaterial)UnityEngine.Object.DestroyImmediate(controlMaterial);foreach(var m in materials)if(m)UnityEngine.Object.DestroyImmediate(m);}
}

 // Homography335 stores a 3x3 projective transform in a Matrix4x4 whose
 // fourth row/column are zero. Embed the independent homogeneous dimension
 // before inversion; Matrix4x4.inverse on the original singular 4x4 is invalid.
 static Matrix4x4 Forward(Matrix4x4 inverse){inverse.m33=1;if(Mathf.Abs(inverse.determinant)<1e-12f)throw new InvalidOperationException("Native source homography singular");return inverse.inverse;}
 void Bind(Material mat,int layer,int owner,Vector2 anchor,Matrix4x4 canonical,int control,bool gpu){
  var f=eye.full;var cam=f.motion.camera;mat.SetInt("_Control89",control);mat.SetInt("_UseBank89",control==0?1:0);mat.SetInt("_SelectedOwner89",owner);mat.SetMatrix("_PhysicalCropGPUVP89",Clip(gpu));mat.SetMatrix("_PhysicalView89",cam.worldToCameraMatrix);mat.SetMatrix("_WorldToHead348",eye.headMatrix.inverse);mat.SetVector("_PhysicalCamera89",new Vector4(cam.transform.position.x,cam.transform.position.y,cam.transform.position.z,cam.orthographic?1:0));mat.SetVector("_PhysicalForward89",cam.transform.forward);mat.SetVector("_SourcePhysicalROI89",new Vector4(eye.roi.x,272-eye.roi.y-eye.roi.height,eye.roi.width,eye.roi.height));mat.SetVector("_SourcePhysicalSize89",new Vector4(eye.roi.width*4,eye.roi.height*4,0,0));mat.SetVector("_CanonicalAnchor89",anchor);mat.SetMatrix("_CanonicalGPUProjection89",canonical);mat.SetInt("_SupportKind348",layer);mat.SetVector("_Profile333",f.motion.animation.shadeProfile);mat.SetVector("_SurfaceInvRaster335",new Vector4(1f/480,1f/272,0,0));mat.SetFloat("_SurfaceOwnShade335",f.motion.animation.shadeProfile.x);mat.SetFloat("_ShadeLayer335",f.shadeLayer?1:0);mat.SetFloat("_SurfaceShadeSteps335",f.surfaceShadeSteps);mat.SetTexture("_FeatureBankRGB89",f.templateRGBA);mat.SetTexture("_FeatureBankRole89",f.templateRole);
  mat.SetInt("_SupportKind348",layer==1?2:layer==2?1:layer);if(owner>0){int i=Array.FindIndex(eye.centres,c=>Mathf.RoundToInt(c.w)==owner);mat.SetVector("_NativeSourceX89",projection[owner][0]);mat.SetVector("_NativeSourceY89",projection[owner][1]);mat.SetMatrix("_AuthoredToHead89",Forward(f.eyeInverse[i]));}
 }

 public RTHandle[][] Handles96=>wrappers;
 public Vector2 Anchor96{get;private set;}public Vector4 Bounds96{get;private set;}
 RTHandle[][] wrappers;RTHandle[] depthWrappers;RTHandle[] controlHandles;RTHandle controlDepth;Material controlMaterial;
 public RenderTexture[] controlFields;public int RenderedFrame{get;private set;}=-1;public int ControlFrame{get;private set;}=-1;
 sealed class Data{public NativeDense107 owner;public int part;}
 void ReleaseWrappers(){if(wrappers!=null)foreach(var a in wrappers)foreach(var x in a)x?.Release();if(depthWrappers!=null)foreach(var x in depthWrappers)x?.Release();wrappers=null;depthWrappers=null;}
 void Wrap(){if(wrappers!=null)return;wrappers=layers.Select(a=>a.Select(t=>RTHandles.Alloc(t,transferOwnership:false)).ToArray()).ToArray();depthWrappers=depths.Select(t=>RTHandles.Alloc(t,transferOwnership:false)).ToArray();}
 void ControlTargets(){int w=eye.roi.width*4,h=eye.roi.height*4;if(controlHandles!=null&&controlFields[0].width==w&&controlFields[0].height==h)return;if(controlHandles!=null)foreach(var t in controlHandles)t?.Release();controlDepth?.Release();controlHandles=new RTHandle[5];controlFields=new RenderTexture[5];for(int i=0;i<5;i++){controlHandles[i]=RTHandles.Alloc(w,h,colorFormat:GraphicsFormat.R32G32B32A32_SFloat,filterMode:FilterMode.Point,name:"95 source projected-coordinate control");controlFields[i]=controlHandles[i].rt;}controlDepth=RTHandles.Alloc(w,h,depthBufferBits:DepthBits.Depth32);if(!controlMaterial){var src=eye.materials[10];controlMaterial=new Material(src){shader=AssetDatabase.LoadAssetAtPath<Shader>(SurfaceAsset),hideFlags=HideFlags.DontSave};controlMaterial.shaderKeywords=src.shaderKeywords;}}
 public void RecordGraph(RenderGraph graph,UniversalResourceData resources){
  if(Disposed||!eye||eye.sampleFace!=sourceMesh)throw new InvalidOperationException("95 source/view lifetime changed");
  var f=eye.full;var current=new Vector2[2];var all=new List<Vector2>();for(int i=0;i<2;i++){int owner=Mathf.RoundToInt(eye.centres[i].w);var h=Forward(f.eyeInverse[i]);Vector2 Project(Vector2 p){var q=h*new Vector4(p.x,p.y,1,0);return new Vector2(q.x/q.z,q.y/q.z);}current[i]=Project(anchors[owner]);foreach(var p in f.contours.eyes[i].parent)all.Add(Project(p));}
  var anchor=(current[0]+current[1])*.5f;var min=new Vector2(Mathf.Floor(all.Min(p=>p.x)-anchor.x)-2,Mathf.Floor(all.Min(p=>p.y)-anchor.y)-2);var max=new Vector2(Mathf.Ceil(all.Max(p=>p.x)-anchor.x)+2,Mathf.Ceil(all.Max(p=>p.y)-anchor.y)+2);int w=Mathf.RoundToInt((max.x-min.x)*8),height=Mathf.RoundToInt((max.y-min.y)*8);if(w*height>32768)throw new InvalidOperationException("95 viewport budget");Targets(w,height);Wrap();if(diagnostics)ControlTargets();var cam=f.motion.camera;var canonical=GL.GetGPUProjectionMatrix(Matrix4x4.Ortho(min.x,max.x,-max.y,-min.y,cam.nearClipPlane,cam.farClipPlane),true);
  Anchor96=anchor;Bounds96=new Vector4(min.x,min.y,max.x,max.y);screenSign=-1;if(diagnostics){Bind(controlMaterial,3,0,Vector2.zero,Matrix4x4.identity,1,true);controlMaterial.SetFloat("_ScreenSign89",screenSign);}
  for(int part=0;part<4;part++)for(int i=0;i<2;i++){var mat=materials[part*2+i];Bind(mat,part,Mathf.RoundToInt(eye.centres[i].w),anchor,canonical,0,true);mat.SetFloat("_ScreenSign89",screenSign);}
  for(int group=diagnostics?4:3;group>=0;group--){using var builder=graph.AddUnsafePass<Data>("95 source-local coupled parts with real graph shadows "+group,out var data);data.owner=this;data.part=group;var colours=group==4?controlHandles:wrappers[group];var depth=group==4?controlDepth:depthWrappers[group];foreach(var t in colours)builder.UseTexture(graph.ImportTexture(t),AccessFlags.Write);builder.UseTexture(graph.ImportTexture(depth),AccessFlags.Write);builder.UseAllGlobalTextures(true);if(resources.mainShadowsTexture.IsValid())builder.UseTexture(resources.mainShadowsTexture);if(resources.additionalShadowsTexture.IsValid())builder.UseTexture(resources.additionalShadowsTexture);builder.AllowPassCulling(false);builder.AllowGlobalStateModification(true);builder.SetRenderFunc(static(Data d,UnsafeGraphContext c)=>d.owner.DrawGraph(d.part,c));}
  Arr58 M(Matrix4x4 m)=>new Arr58(Enumerable.Range(0,16).Select(i=>(double)m[i]));Info["anchor"]=new Arr58(anchor.x,anchor.y);Info["bounds"]=new Arr58(min.x,min.y,max.x,max.y);Info["size"]=new Arr58(w,height);Info["canonicalGPUProjection"]=M(canonical);Info["physicalView"]=M(cam.worldToCameraMatrix);Info["headMatrix"]=M(eye.headMatrix);Info["parts"]=new Arr58(parts);Info["sampler"]=8;Info["sourceFrame"]=f.motion.sourceFrame;Info["independentMaterialsPerPartAndEye"]=true;Info["graphContext"]=true;Info["borrowedTextureOwnershipTransferred"]=false;
 }
 void DrawGraph(int part,UnsafeGraphContext context){var cmd=CommandBufferHelpers.GetNativeCommandBuffer(context.cmd);bool control=part==4;var textures=control?controlFields:layers[part];cmd.SetRenderTarget(textures.Select(t=>new RenderTargetIdentifier(t)).ToArray(),control?controlDepth.rt:depths[part]);cmd.ClearRenderTarget(RTClearFlags.All,Color.clear,1,0);cmd.SetViewport(new Rect(0,0,textures[0].width,textures[0].height));var model=eye.full.motion.surface.outputs[1].transform.localToWorldMatrix;if(control){cmd.DrawMesh(sourceMesh,model,controlMaterial,6,0);ControlFrame=Time.frameCount;}else{for(int i=0;i<2;i++)cmd.DrawMesh(sourceMesh,model,materials[part*2+i],parts[part],0);if(part==0)RenderedFrame=Time.frameCount;}}
 public void AssertRendered(){if(RenderedFrame!=Time.frameCount||(diagnostics&&ControlFrame!=Time.frameCount))throw new InvalidOperationException("95 source graph pass missing; no fallback draw");}
 public void Dispose(){if(Disposed)return;Disposed=true;ReleaseTargets();if(controlHandles!=null)foreach(var t in controlHandles)t?.Release();controlDepth?.Release();if(controlMaterial)UnityEngine.Object.DestroyImmediate(controlMaterial);foreach(var m in materials)if(m)UnityEngine.Object.DestroyImmediate(m);}
}



// Explicit runtime view/owned materials/owned RTs. Never writes globals, borrowed
// source geometry, original shaders/materials, InputSettings or product output.
public sealed class NativeCarrier113:IDisposable{
 public const string SurfaceAsset="Assets/EUnityValidation01/EyeBoundaryRestore348/Shaders/NativeCarrier111.shader";
 public const string SkinAsset="Assets/EUnityValidation01/EyeBoundaryRestore348/Shaders/NativeSkin89.shader";
 readonly bool diagnostics;readonly EyeFeature348 eye;readonly UnityEngine.Mesh sourceMesh;readonly Material[] materials=new Material[8];
 readonly int[] parts={3,1,4,6};readonly Dictionary<int,Vector4[]> projection=new Dictionary<int,Vector4[]>();
 readonly Dictionary<int,Vector2> anchors=new Dictionary<int,Vector2>();
 public RenderTexture[][] layers;public RenderTexture[] depths;public Obj58 Info{get;private set;}
 public bool Disposed{get;private set;}public int currentWidth,currentHeight;int controlConvention=-1;float screenSign=-1;
 // Json58 has int and double conversion operators but no float operator.
 // A direct (float)Json58 selects its int conversion and rounds the source.
 static float F(Json58 number)=>(float)(double)number;
 public NativeCarrier113(EyeFeature348 e,string registrationFile,string diagnosticFile,bool fullDiagnostics=false){diagnostics=fullDiagnostics;
  eye=e;sourceMesh=e.sampleFace;if(!eye||!eye.full||!sourceMesh||EditorUtility.IsPersistent(eye))throw new InvalidOperationException("Live prepared source view required");
  var reg=Json58.Parse(File.ReadAllText(registrationFile));
  foreach(var row in reg["eyes"]){int owner=(int)row["eye"];var P=row["nativeToAuthoredProjection"];projection[owner]=new[]{new Vector4(F(P[0][0]),F(P[1][0]),F(P[2][0]),F(P[3][0])),new Vector4(F(P[0][1]),F(P[1][1]),F(P[2][1]),F(P[3][1]))};var cells=reg["cells"].Where(c=>(int)c["eye"]==owner).ToArray();float minX=cells.Min(c=>F(c["sourceXY"][0])),maxX=cells.Max(c=>F(c["sourceXY"][0])),minY=cells.Min(c=>F(c["sourceXY"][1])),maxY=cells.Max(c=>F(c["sourceXY"][1]));anchors[owner]=new Vector2(Mathf.Floor((minX+maxX)/2)+.5f,Mathf.Floor((minY+maxY)/2)+.5f);}
  var rest=new List<Vector3>();sourceMesh.GetUVs(1,rest);float error=0;var measured=new Arr58();
  foreach(var row in reg["eyes"]){int owner=(int)row["eye"];var ids=row["nativeRingVertexIds"];for(int i=0;i<ids.Count;i++){var v=rest[(int)ids[i]];var q=new Vector4(v.x,v.y,v.z,1);var target=row["authoredParent"][i];var actual=new Vector2(Vector4.Dot(q,projection[owner][0]),Vector4.Dot(q,projection[owner][1]));error=Mathf.Max(error,Vector2.Distance(actual,new Vector2(F(target[0]),F(target[1]))));measured.Add(new Obj58{["owner"]=owner,["ringIndex"]=i,["vertex"]=(int)ids[i],["rest"]=new Arr58(v.x,v.y,v.z),["projected"]=new Arr58(actual.x,actual.y),["target"]=target});}}
  CreateOnly40.Text(diagnosticFile,new Obj58{["maxError"]=error,["headBoneIndex"]=eye.headBoneIndex,["sourceTopology"]=eye.modelTopologySHA256,["sourceMesh"]=sourceMesh.name,["sourceMeshId"]=sourceMesh.GetEntityId().ToString(),["uv1Count"]=rest.Count,["sourceToRestHead"]=new Arr58(Enumerable.Range(0,16).Select(i=>(double)eye.sourceToRestHead[i])),["projection"]=new Arr58(projection.Select(p=>new Obj58{["owner"]=p.Key,["x"]=new Arr58(p.Value[0].x,p.Value[0].y,p.Value[0].z,p.Value[0].w),["y"]=new Arr58(p.Value[1].x,p.Value[1].y,p.Value[1].z,p.Value[1].w)})),["measurements"]=measured}.ToString());
  if(error>1e-3)throw new InvalidOperationException("Current source rest/boundary registration mismatch "+error);
  try{for(int i=0;i<8;i++){int role=parts[i/2];var src=role==3?eye.skinMaterial:eye.materials[4+role];var shader=AssetDatabase.LoadAssetAtPath<Shader>(role==3?SkinAsset:SurfaceAsset);if(!src||!shader||!shader.isSupported)throw new InvalidOperationException("Retarget source shader/material unavailable");var keywords=src.shaderKeywords;materials[i]=new Material(src){shader=shader,hideFlags=HideFlags.DontSave,name="95 owned native source part "+role+" eye-slot "+(i%2)};materials[i].shaderKeywords=keywords;}}
  catch{Dispose();throw;}
  Info=new Obj58{["registrationMaxPixels"]=error,["sourceMeshId"]=sourceMesh.GetEntityId().ToString(),["borrowedMeshMutated"]=false,["globalWrites"]=0,["productBindingsChanged"]=false};
 }
 RenderTexture Colour(int w,int h,string name){var d=new RenderTextureDescriptor(w,h){graphicsFormat=GraphicsFormat.R32G32B32A32_SFloat,depthStencilFormat=GraphicsFormat.None,msaaSamples=1,sRGB=false};var t=new RenderTexture(d){filterMode=FilterMode.Point,hideFlags=HideFlags.DontSave,name=name};if(!t.Create()){UnityEngine.Object.DestroyImmediate(t);throw new InvalidOperationException("Own colour RT creation failed");}return t;}
 RenderTexture Depth(int w,int h){var d=new RenderTextureDescriptor(w,h){graphicsFormat=GraphicsFormat.None,depthStencilFormat=GraphicsFormat.D32_SFloat,msaaSamples=1};var t=new RenderTexture(d){filterMode=FilterMode.Point,hideFlags=HideFlags.DontSave,name="89 owned depth"};if(!t.Create()){UnityEngine.Object.DestroyImmediate(t);throw new InvalidOperationException("Own depth RT creation failed");}return t;}
 void ReleaseTargets(){ReleaseWrappers();if(layers!=null)foreach(var a in layers)if(a!=null)foreach(var t in a)if(t){t.Release();UnityEngine.Object.DestroyImmediate(t);}if(depths!=null)foreach(var t in depths)if(t){t.Release();UnityEngine.Object.DestroyImmediate(t);}layers=null;depths=null;}
 void Targets(int w,int h){if(layers!=null&&w==currentWidth&&h==currentHeight)return;ReleaseTargets();currentWidth=w;currentHeight=h;layers=new RenderTexture[4][];depths=new RenderTexture[4];try{for(int i=0;i<4;i++){layers[i]=new RenderTexture[5];for(int ch=0;ch<5;ch++)layers[i][ch]=Colour(w,h,"89 source "+parts[i]+" channel "+ch);depths[i]=Depth(w,h);}}catch{ReleaseTargets();throw;}}
 Matrix4x4 Clip(bool gpu){var cam=eye.full.motion.camera;return(gpu?GL.GetGPUProjectionMatrix(eye.cropProjection,true):eye.cropProjection)*cam.worldToCameraMatrix;}
 // Homography335 stores a 3x3 projective transform in a Matrix4x4 whose
 // fourth row/column are zero. Embed the independent homogeneous dimension
 // before inversion; Matrix4x4.inverse on the original singular 4x4 is invalid.
 static Matrix4x4 Forward(Matrix4x4 inverse){inverse.m33=1;if(Mathf.Abs(inverse.determinant)<1e-12f)throw new InvalidOperationException("Native source homography singular");return inverse.inverse;}
 void Bind(Material mat,int layer,int owner,Vector2 anchor,Matrix4x4 canonical,int control,bool gpu){
  var f=eye.full;var cam=f.motion.camera;mat.SetInt("_Control89",control);mat.SetInt("_UseBank89",0);mat.SetInt("_CarrierGeometryOnly111",1);mat.SetInt("_SelectedOwner89",owner);mat.SetMatrix("_PhysicalCropGPUVP89",Clip(gpu));mat.SetMatrix("_PhysicalView89",cam.worldToCameraMatrix);mat.SetMatrix("_WorldToHead348",eye.headMatrix.inverse);mat.SetVector("_PhysicalCamera89",new Vector4(cam.transform.position.x,cam.transform.position.y,cam.transform.position.z,cam.orthographic?1:0));mat.SetVector("_PhysicalForward89",cam.transform.forward);mat.SetVector("_SourcePhysicalROI89",new Vector4(eye.roi.x,272-eye.roi.y-eye.roi.height,eye.roi.width,eye.roi.height));mat.SetVector("_SourcePhysicalSize89",new Vector4(eye.roi.width*4,eye.roi.height*4,0,0));mat.SetVector("_CanonicalAnchor89",anchor);mat.SetMatrix("_CanonicalGPUProjection89",canonical);mat.SetInt("_SupportKind348",layer);mat.SetVector("_Profile333",f.motion.animation.shadeProfile);mat.SetVector("_SurfaceInvRaster335",new Vector4(1f/480,1f/272,0,0));mat.SetFloat("_SurfaceOwnShade335",f.motion.animation.shadeProfile.x);mat.SetFloat("_ShadeLayer335",f.shadeLayer?1:0);mat.SetFloat("_SurfaceShadeSteps335",f.surfaceShadeSteps);mat.SetTexture("_FeatureBankRGB89",f.templateRGBA);mat.SetTexture("_FeatureBankRole89",f.templateRole);
  mat.SetInt("_SupportKind348",layer==1?2:layer==2?1:layer);if(owner>0){int i=Array.FindIndex(eye.centres,c=>Mathf.RoundToInt(c.w)==owner);mat.SetVector("_NativeSourceX89",projection[owner][0]);mat.SetVector("_NativeSourceY89",projection[owner][1]);mat.SetMatrix("_AuthoredToHead89",Forward(f.eyeInverse[i]));}
 }

// Source binding belongs to this renderer/view. Static mesh/material/UV topology
// is read once; only already-evaluated CPU mesh vertices are uploaded each frame.
// No BakeMesh, GPU readback, source mutation or replacement of InputSettings.
public sealed class LiveSurface107:IDisposable{
 readonly Full319View335 view;readonly Camera sourceCamera;readonly SkinnedMeshRenderer[] sourceRenderers;readonly EyeFeature348 eye;readonly MeshFilter[] outputs;readonly UnityEngine.Mesh[] meshes;readonly List<Vector3>[] scratch;readonly Vector4[] world;readonly string topology;readonly string templateId;readonly Material[][] nativeMaterials;readonly int[] starts;public WholeEye107 Engine{get;private set;}public string Scope{get;private set;}bool disposed;
 public LiveSurface107(EyeFeature348 e,ComputeShader shader,int[] ids,Vector4[] px,Vector4[] py){
  eye=e;var f=e.full;view=f;sourceCamera=f.motion.camera;sourceRenderers=f.motion.surface.source;outputs=f.motion.surface.outputs;var source=f.motion.surface.source;if(outputs.Length!=source.Length||outputs.Length>8)throw new InvalidOperationException("Explicit renderer source relation required");meshes=new UnityEngine.Mesh[outputs.Length];scratch=new List<Vector3>[outputs.Length];starts=new int[outputs.Length];nativeMaterials=new Material[outputs.Length][];topology=e.modelTopologySHA256;templateId=f.templateRole.GetEntityId().ToString();Scope=e.GetEntityId()+"/"+f.motion.camera.GetEntityId()+"/"+topology+"/"+templateId;
  var uv=new List<Vector2>();var rest=new List<Vector4>();var positions=new List<Vector4>();var tris=new List<WholeEye107.Triangle>();var matProps=new List<WholeEye107.MaterialData>();var alphaTextures=new List<Texture2D>();var alphaIndices=new Dictionary<Texture2D,int>();var maps=new List<int>[] {new List<int>(),new List<int>(),new List<int>(),new List<int>()};int[] parts={3,1,4,6};var restFace=new List<Vector3>();e.sampleFace.GetUVs(1,restFace);var ownerFace=new List<Vector2>();e.sampleFace.GetUVs(7,ownerFace);if(restFace.Count!=e.sampleFace.vertexCount||ownerFace.Count!=restFace.Count)throw new InvalidOperationException("Existing source rest/owner contract missing");
  for(int slot=0;slot<outputs.Length;slot++){
   var mesh=slot==1?e.sampleFace:outputs[slot].sharedMesh;if(!mesh||!mesh.isReadable)throw new InvalidOperationException("Existing evaluated CPU-readable source required");meshes[slot]=mesh;scratch[slot]=new List<Vector3>(mesh.vertexCount);mesh.GetVertices(scratch[slot]);starts[slot]=positions.Count;nativeMaterials[slot]=source[slot].sharedMaterials;var u=new List<Vector2>();mesh.GetUVs(0,u);if(u.Count!=mesh.vertexCount)throw new InvalidOperationException("Source UV missing");uv.AddRange(u);for(int i=0;i<mesh.vertexCount;i++){Vector3 p=outputs[slot].transform.localToWorldMatrix.MultiplyPoint3x4(scratch[slot][i]);positions.Add(new Vector4(p.x,p.y,p.z,1));var r=slot==1?restFace[i]:Vector3.zero;rest.Add(new Vector4(r.x,r.y,r.z,1));}
   for(int sm=0;sm<mesh.subMeshCount;sm++){
    if(sm>=nativeMaterials[slot].Length)throw new InvalidOperationException("Material/submesh source gap");var mat=nativeMaterials[slot][sm];int mi=matProps.Count;var tex=mat.GetTexture("_MainTex")as Texture2D;int mode=mat.IsKeywordEnabled("_ALPHATEST_ON")?1:mat.IsKeywordEnabled("_ALPHABLEND_ON")?2:0;
    if(!tex||tex.mipmapCount!=1||tex.filterMode!=FilterMode.Bilinear||tex.anisoLevel>1||mat.mainTextureScale!=Vector2.one||mat.mainTextureOffset!=Vector2.zero)throw new NotSupportedException("Source sampler changed: no silent approximation");foreach(string key in new[]{"_UvAnimScrollXSpeed","_UvAnimScrollYSpeed","_UvAnimRotationSpeed"})if(mat.HasProperty(key)&&mat.GetFloat(key)!=0)throw new NotSupportedException("Animated UV needs explicit producer");int Wrap(TextureWrapMode w){if(w==TextureWrapMode.Repeat)return 0;if(w==TextureWrapMode.Clamp)return 1;throw new NotSupportedException("Mirror wrapping not implemented");}int ti=0;if(mode>0&&!alphaIndices.TryGetValue(tex,out ti)){ti=alphaTextures.Count;alphaIndices.Add(tex,ti);alphaTextures.Add(tex);}matProps.Add(new WholeEye107.MaterialData{atlas=new Vector4(ti,tex.width,tex.height,mode),alphaCullWrap=new Vector4(mat.GetColor("_Color").a,mat.GetFloat("_Cutoff"),mat.GetFloat("_M_CullMode"),Wrap(tex.wrapModeU)),uvWrap=new Vector4(Wrap(tex.wrapModeV),1,1,0)});
    int[] ix=mesh.GetIndices(sm);if(ix.Length%3!=0)throw new InvalidOperationException("Triangle topology required");for(int i=0;i<ix.Length;i+=3){int triangle=tris.Count;var owners=slot==1?new Vector4(ownerFace[ix[i]].x,ownerFace[ix[i+1]].x,ownerFace[ix[i+2]].x,0):Vector4.zero;tris.Add(new WholeEye107.Triangle{v=new WholeEye107.I4(starts[slot]+ix[i],starts[slot]+ix[i+1],starts[slot]+ix[i+2],mi),source=new WholeEye107.I4(slot,sm,i/3+1,0),eye=owners});if(slot==1&&Array.IndexOf(parts,sm)>=0)maps[Array.IndexOf(parts,sm)].Add(triangle);}
   }
  }
  int stride=maps.Max(x=>x.Count);var mapping=Enumerable.Repeat(-1,stride*4).ToArray();for(int p=0;p<4;p++)maps[p].CopyTo(mapping,p*stride);world=positions.ToArray();
  try{Engine=new WholeEye107(shader,Scope,tris.ToArray(),uv.ToArray(),rest.ToArray(),mapping,stride,world,matProps.ToArray(),1,ids,px,py);Engine.SetAlphaSources(alphaTextures.ToArray());}catch{Dispose();throw;}
 }
 public void Update(){if(disposed)throw new ObjectDisposedException(nameof(LiveSurface107));if(!eye||eye.full!=view||view.motion.camera!=sourceCamera||!System.Object.ReferenceEquals(view.motion.surface.outputs,outputs)||!System.Object.ReferenceEquals(view.motion.surface.source,sourceRenderers)||eye.modelTopologySHA256!=topology||eye.full.templateRole.GetEntityId().ToString()!=templateId||eye.sampleFace!=meshes[1])throw new InvalidOperationException("Model/template/source lifetime changed; new explicit owner required");for(int slot=0;slot<outputs.Length;slot++){if(!outputs[slot]||outputs[slot].sharedMesh.vertexCount!=meshes[slot].vertexCount||(slot!=1&&outputs[slot].sharedMesh!=meshes[slot]))throw new InvalidOperationException("Evaluated source changed");meshes[slot].GetVertices(scratch[slot]);var matrix=outputs[slot].transform.localToWorldMatrix;for(int i=0;i<scratch[slot].Count;i++){var v=matrix.MultiplyPoint3x4(scratch[slot][i]);world[starts[slot]+i]=new Vector4(v.x,v.y,v.z,1);}}Engine.UploadWorld(world);}
 public void SourceFeaturesEditedInPlace(){if(disposed)throw new ObjectDisposedException(nameof(LiveSurface107));Engine.InvalidateWitness();}
 public void Dispose(){if(disposed)return;disposed=true;Engine?.Dispose();}
}



// One instance owns one model/view, source atlas, topology BVH and witness cache.
// No static mutable state, borrowed buffer disposal, readback or asset lookup.
public sealed class WholeEye107:IDisposable{
 public struct I4{public int x,y,z,w;public I4(int a,int b,int c,int d){x=a;y=b;z=c;w=d;}}
 public struct Triangle{public I4 v,source;public Vector4 eye;}
 public struct MaterialData{public Vector4 atlas,alphaCullWrap,uvWrap;}
 public readonly string Scope;public readonly ComputeShader Shader;public readonly int VertexCount,TriangleCount,MaterialCount,PrimitiveStride;
 public GraphicsBuffer Evidence107{get;private set;} readonly Triangle[] cpuTriangles107;readonly Vector2[] cpuUV107;readonly Vector4[] cpuRest107;readonly MaterialData[] cpuMaterials107;readonly int[] cpuParts107;Vector4[] cpuWorld107;
 public GraphicsBuffer World{get;private set;}public GraphicsBuffer Layers{get;private set;}public GraphicsBuffer Queries{get;private set;}public GraphicsBuffer Visibility{get;private set;}public GraphicsBuffer Colour{get;private set;}public GraphicsBuffer Identity{get;private set;}public GraphicsBuffer Decisions{get;private set;}public GraphicsBuffer Witness{get;private set;}public GraphicsBuffer Stats{get;private set;}
 readonly GraphicsBuffer triangles,uv,rest,parts,nodes,levelNodes,boxes,materials;readonly int[] levelOffsets,levelCounts;Texture2D[] alphaSources;readonly uint[] zeroStats=new uint[8];readonly int[] eyeIds;readonly Vector4[] projectionX,projectionY;
 readonly List<GraphicsBuffer> owned=new List<GraphicsBuffer>();int width,height,nodeCount;bool disposed;public int Rebuilds{get;private set;}public int Frames{get;private set;}public long OwnedBytes{get;private set;}
 public IEnumerable<GraphicsBuffer> Buffers=>owned;
 public bool Disposed=>disposed;public int Width=>width;public int Height=>height;
 GraphicsBuffer B(int n,int stride){if(n<1)throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException();var b=new GraphicsBuffer(GraphicsBuffer.Target.Structured,n,stride);owned.Add(b);OwnedBytes+=(long)n*stride;return b;}
 void Drop(GraphicsBuffer b){if(b==null)return;if(owned.Remove(b)){OwnedBytes-=(long)b.count*b.stride;b.Dispose();}}
 public WholeEye107(ComputeShader original,string scope,Triangle[] sourceTriangles,Vector2[] sourceUV,Vector4[] sourceRest,int[] partMap,int primitiveStride,Vector4[] initialWorld,MaterialData[] sourceMaterials,int alphaCount,int[] sourceEyeIds,Vector4[] px,Vector4[] py){
  if(!original||string.IsNullOrEmpty(scope)||sourceTriangles.Length>262144||initialWorld.Length>131072||sourceUV.Length!=initialWorld.Length||sourceRest.Length!=initialWorld.Length||partMap.Length!=primitiveStride*4||alphaCount>16777216)throw new ArgumentException("Bounded source topology contract");
  global::Execution24.DispatchBounds24.Topology(initialWorld.Length,sourceTriangles.Length,sourceMaterials.Length,primitiveStride,partMap.Length,alphaCount);
  if(sourceEyeIds==null||sourceEyeIds.Length!=2||sourceEyeIds[0]<1||sourceEyeIds[0]>=32||sourceEyeIds[1]<1||sourceEyeIds[1]>=32||sourceEyeIds[0]==sourceEyeIds[1]||px==null||py==null||px.Length!=2||py.Length!=2)throw new ArgumentException("24 eye/projection identities");
  foreach(var t in sourceTriangles)global::Execution24.DispatchBounds24.Triangle(t.v.x,t.v.y,t.v.z,t.v.w,initialWorld.Length,sourceMaterials.Length);
  foreach(int t in partMap)global::Execution24.DispatchBounds24.PartIndex(t,sourceTriangles.Length);
  foreach(var a in initialWorld)for(int i=0;i<4;i++)global::Execution24.DispatchBounds24.Finite(a[i]);foreach(var a in sourceRest)for(int i=0;i<4;i++)global::Execution24.DispatchBounds24.Finite(a[i]);foreach(var a in sourceUV){global::Execution24.DispatchBounds24.Finite(a.x);global::Execution24.DispatchBounds24.Finite(a.y);}
  foreach(var a in px.Concat(py))for(int i=0;i<4;i++)global::Execution24.DispatchBounds24.Finite(a[i]);foreach(var m in sourceMaterials){global::Execution24.DispatchBounds24.Texture(m.atlas.x,m.atlas.y,m.atlas.z,m.atlas.w);for(int i=0;i<4;i++){global::Execution24.DispatchBounds24.Finite(m.alphaCullWrap[i]);global::Execution24.DispatchBounds24.Finite(m.uvWrap[i]);}}
  cpuTriangles107=sourceTriangles;cpuUV107=sourceUV;cpuRest107=sourceRest;cpuMaterials107=sourceMaterials;cpuParts107=partMap;cpuWorld107=initialWorld;Scope=scope;VertexCount=initialWorld.Length;TriangleCount=sourceTriangles.Length;MaterialCount=sourceMaterials.Length;PrimitiveStride=primitiveStride;eyeIds=(int[])sourceEyeIds.Clone();projectionX=(Vector4[])px.Clone();projectionY=(Vector4[])py.Clone();
  Shader=UnityEngine.Object.Instantiate(original);Shader.hideFlags=HideFlags.DontSave;
  try{
   triangles=B(TriangleCount,48);triangles.SetData(sourceTriangles);World=B(VertexCount,16);World.SetData(initialWorld);uv=B(VertexCount,8);uv.SetData(sourceUV);rest=B(VertexCount,16);rest.SetData(sourceRest);parts=B(partMap.Length,4);parts.SetData(partMap);materials=B(MaterialCount,48);materials.SetData(sourceMaterials);
   var centres=new Vector3[TriangleCount];for(int i=0;i<TriangleCount;i++){var t=sourceTriangles[i].v;centres[i]=(initialWorld[t.x]+initialWorld[t.y]+initialWorld[t.z])/3f;}
   var ns=new List<I4>();var depths=new List<List<int>>();
   int Build(int[] ids,int depth){int at=ns.Count;ns.Add(new I4());while(depths.Count<=depth)depths.Add(new List<int>());depths[depth].Add(at);if(ids.Length==1){ns[at]=new I4(-1,-1,ids[0],depth);return at;}var min=centres[ids[0]];var max=min;foreach(int i in ids){min=Vector3.Min(min,centres[i]);max=Vector3.Max(max,centres[i]);}var extent=max-min;int axis=extent.x>=extent.y&&extent.x>=extent.z?0:extent.y>=extent.z?1:2;Array.Sort(ids,(a,b)=>{int c=centres[a][axis].CompareTo(centres[b][axis]);return c!=0?c:a.CompareTo(b);});int middle=ids.Length/2;int left=Build(ids.Take(middle).ToArray(),depth+1),right=Build(ids.Skip(middle).ToArray(),depth+1);ns[at]=new I4(left,right,-1,depth);return at;}
   Build(Enumerable.Range(0,TriangleCount).ToArray(),0);if(depths.Count>30)throw new InvalidOperationException("BVH stack contract");nodeCount=ns.Count;nodes=B(ns.Count,16);nodes.SetData(ns);boxes=B(ns.Count*2,16);var flat=new List<int>();levelOffsets=new int[depths.Count];levelCounts=new int[depths.Count];for(int i=depths.Count-1;i>=0;i--){levelOffsets[i]=flat.Count;levelCounts[i]=depths[i].Count;flat.AddRange(depths[i]);}levelNodes=B(flat.Count,4);levelNodes.SetData(flat);Stats=B(8,4);Rebuilds=1;
  }catch{Dispose();throw;}
 }

 public void Resize(int w,int h,string scope){Check();if(scope!=Scope)throw new InvalidOperationException("Model/view/generation changed: dispose and create a new owner");global::Execution24.DispatchBounds24.Product(w,h,4,32768);if(width==w&&height==h)return;foreach(var b in new[]{Layers,Queries,Visibility,Colour,Identity,Decisions,Witness,Evidence107})Drop(b);width=w;height=h;int n=w*h,c=n/16;Evidence107=B(n*8,16);Layers=B(n*20,16);Queries=B(n*4,16);Visibility=B(n*4,16);Colour=B(c,16);Identity=B(c,16);Decisions=B(c,16);Witness=B(c,16);Witness.SetData(new I4[c]);}
 public void InvalidateWitness(){Check();if(Witness!=null)Witness.SetData(new I4[Witness.count]);}
 public sealed class Composition{
  public Texture baseColour,baseOwner,baseDepth,oldOwner,oldIntrinsic,backingColour,backingMeta,backingUV;
  public ComputeBuffer records;public Vector4 nativeROI,oldROI,backingROI,recordROI,originScale,joinDelta,anchorBounds;
  public Texture nonEyeColour98,nonEyeMeta98,nonEyeUV98,worldColour98,worldProof98;public bool useUnderlay98,useWorld98;
  public float depthOffset;public bool cropped;public RenderTexture[] output;
 }
 public void Compose(CommandBuffer cmd,Composition c){
  Check();if(c.records==null||c.output==null||c.output.Length!=4)throw new ArgumentException("Explicit current composition inputs");int k=Shader.FindKernel("Compose");cmd.SetComputeIntParams(Shader,"_Size96",width,height);
  void R(string name,Vector4 r)=>cmd.SetComputeIntParams(Shader,name,(int)r.x,(int)r.y,(int)r.z,(int)r.w);
  R("_NativeROI96",c.nativeROI);R("_OldROI96",c.oldROI);R("_BackingROI96",c.backingROI);R("_RecordROI96",c.recordROI);cmd.SetComputeIntParam(Shader,"_CroppedInputs96",c.cropped?1:0);cmd.SetComputeVectorParam(Shader,"_NativeOriginScale96",c.originScale);cmd.SetComputeVectorParam(Shader,"_JoinDelta96",c.joinDelta);cmd.SetComputeVectorParam(Shader,"_AnchorBounds96",c.anchorBounds);cmd.SetComputeFloatParam(Shader,"_DepthOffset96",c.depthOffset);
  string[] names={"_BaseFinal96","_BaseOwner96","_BaseDepth96","_OldOwner96","_OldIntrinsic96","_BackingColour96","_BackingMeta96","_BackingUV96"};Texture[] inputs={c.baseColour,c.baseOwner,c.baseDepth,c.oldOwner,c.oldIntrinsic,c.backingColour,c.backingMeta,c.backingUV};for(int i=0;i<names.Length;i++)cmd.SetComputeTextureParam(Shader,k,names[i],inputs[i]);cmd.SetComputeIntParam(Shader,"_UseUnderlay98",c.useUnderlay98?1:0);cmd.SetComputeIntParam(Shader,"_UseWorld98",c.useWorld98?1:0);string[] n98={"_NonEyeColour98","_NonEyeMeta98","_NonEyeUV98","_PackedWorldColour98","_PackedWorldProof98"};Texture[] t98={c.nonEyeColour98,c.nonEyeMeta98,c.nonEyeUV98,c.worldColour98,c.worldProof98};for(int i=0;i<n98.Length;i++)cmd.SetComputeTextureParam(Shader,k,n98[i],t98[i]?t98[i]:Texture2D.blackTexture);cmd.SetComputeBufferParam(Shader,k,"_Records96",c.records);cmd.SetComputeBufferParam(Shader,k,"_ReadLayers96",Layers);cmd.SetComputeBufferParam(Shader,k,"_ReadVisibility96",Visibility);cmd.SetComputeBufferParam(Shader,k,"_ReadColour96",Colour);cmd.SetComputeBufferParam(Shader,k,"_ReadIdentity96",Identity);cmd.SetComputeBufferParam(Shader,k,"_ReadDecision96",Decisions);for(int i=0;i<4;i++)cmd.SetComputeTextureParam(Shader,k,new[]{"_FinalColour96","_FinalOwner96","_FinalDepth96","_FinalAudit96"}[i],c.output[i]);cmd.DispatchCompute(Shader,k,((int)c.nativeROI.z+7)/8,((int)c.nativeROI.w+7)/8,1);
 }
 public void PackWorld98(CommandBuffer cmd,Texture colour,Texture depth,RenderTexture outColour,RenderTexture outProof,Vector4 roi,Vector4 originScale,Vector2 delta,Vector4 nearFar){Check();int k=Shader.FindKernel("PackWorld98");cmd.BeginSample("WholeEye98 exact world underlay pack");cmd.SetComputeIntParams(Shader,"_NativeROI96",(int)roi.x,(int)roi.y,(int)roi.z,(int)roi.w);cmd.SetComputeVectorParam(Shader,"_NativeOriginScale96",originScale);cmd.SetComputeVectorParam(Shader,"_WorldDelta98",delta);cmd.SetComputeVectorParam(Shader,"_WorldNearFar98",nearFar);cmd.SetComputeTextureParam(Shader,k,"_NativeWorldColour98",colour);cmd.SetComputeTextureParam(Shader,k,"_NativeWorldDepth98",depth);cmd.SetComputeTextureParam(Shader,k,"_WorldColourOut98",outColour);cmd.SetComputeTextureParam(Shader,k,"_WorldProofOut98",outProof);cmd.DispatchCompute(Shader,k,((int)roi.z+7)/8,((int)roi.w+7)/8,1);cmd.EndSample("WholeEye98 exact world underlay pack");}
 public void UploadWorld(Vector4[] data){Check();if(data.Length!=VertexCount)throw new InvalidOperationException("Evaluated topology changed");foreach(var a in data)for(int i=0;i<4;i++)global::Execution24.DispatchBounds24.Finite(a[i]);cpuWorld107=data;World.SetData(data);}
 public void CheckPack24(int part,RenderTexture[] fields){Check();if(part<0||part>3||fields==null||fields.Length!=5)throw new ArgumentException("24 pack input count");for(int i=0;i<5;i++)if(!fields[i]||fields[i].width!=width||fields[i].height!=height)throw new ArgumentException("24 pack texture/grid mismatch");}
 public void Pack(CommandBuffer cmd,int part,RenderTexture[] fields,bool flip){CheckPack24(part,fields);int k=Shader.FindKernel("PackPart");cmd.SetComputeIntParams(Shader,"_Size96",width,height);cmd.SetComputeIntParam(Shader,"_Part96",part);cmd.SetComputeIntParam(Shader,"_FlipY96",flip?1:0);cmd.SetComputeBufferParam(Shader,k,"_Layers96",Layers);string[] names={"_PartRGBA96","_PartMeta96","_PartRest96","_PartUV96","_PartShade96"};for(int i=0;i<5;i++)cmd.SetComputeTextureParam(Shader,k,names[i],fields[i]);cmd.DispatchCompute(Shader,k,(width+7)/8,(height+7)/8,1);}
 public void Dispatch(CommandBuffer cmd,Matrix4x4 view,Matrix4x4 projection,bool directQueries,int coldTie=0,bool selectOutput=true){
  if(selectOutput&&!Shader.HasKernel("Select"))throw new InvalidOperationException("24 selection output unavailable in this isolated shader");CheckMatrices24(view,projection);Check();if(alphaSources==null||alphaSources.Any(t=>!t))throw new InvalidOperationException("Borrowed alpha source lost; explicit reinitialization required");if(Layers==null)throw new InvalidOperationException("Resize before dispatch");cmd.SetBufferData(Stats,zeroStats);cmd.BeginSample("WholeEye107 BVH visibility and joint selection");cmd.SetComputeIntParams(Shader,"_Size96",width,height);cmd.SetComputeMatrixParam(Shader,"_View96",view);cmd.SetComputeMatrixParam(Shader,"_Projection96",projection);cmd.SetComputeIntParam(Shader,"_NodeCount96",nodeCount);cmd.SetComputeIntParam(Shader,"_FacePrimitiveStride96",PrimitiveStride);cmd.SetComputeIntParam(Shader,"_DirectQueries96",directQueries?1:0);cmd.SetComputeIntParam(Shader,"_ColdTie96",coldTie);
  int k=Shader.FindKernel("Refit");void Bind(string name,GraphicsBuffer b)=>cmd.SetComputeBufferParam(Shader,k,name,b);Bind("_World96",World);Bind("_Triangles96",triangles);Bind("_Nodes96",nodes);Bind("_LevelNodes96",levelNodes);Bind("_Boxes96",boxes);for(int i=levelCounts.Length-1;i>=0;i--){cmd.SetComputeIntParam(Shader,"_LevelOffset96",levelOffsets[i]);cmd.SetComputeIntParam(Shader,"_LevelCount96",levelCounts[i]);cmd.DispatchCompute(Shader,k,(levelCounts[i]+63)/64,1,1);}
  k=Shader.FindKernel("Query");Bind("_Triangles96",triangles);Bind("_Materials96",materials);Bind("_World96",World);Bind("_UV96",uv);Bind("_Rest96",rest);Bind("_PartTriangles96",parts);Bind("_Nodes96",nodes);Bind("_Boxes96",boxes);for(int i=0;i<32;i++)cmd.SetComputeTextureParam(Shader,k,"_SourceAlpha96_"+i,i<alphaSources.Length?alphaSources[i]:Texture2D.whiteTexture);Bind("_Layers96",Layers);Bind("_Visibility96",Visibility);Bind("_QueryEvidence107",Evidence107);Bind("_Queries96",Queries);Bind("_Stats96",Stats);cmd.DispatchCompute(Shader,k,(width*height*4+63)/64,1,1);
  if(!selectOutput){cmd.SetComputeVectorArrayParam(Shader,"_SourceProjectionX96",projectionX);cmd.SetComputeVectorArrayParam(Shader,"_SourceProjectionY96",projectionY);cmd.SetComputeIntParams(Shader,"_SourceEyeIds96",eyeIds);cmd.EndSample("WholeEye107 BVH visibility and joint selection");Frames++;return;}
  k=Shader.FindKernel("Select");Bind("_Layers96",Layers);Bind("_Visibility96",Visibility);Bind("_CanonicalColour96",Colour);Bind("_CanonicalIdentity96",Identity);Bind("_Decision96",Decisions);Bind("_Witness96",Witness);Bind("_Stats96",Stats);cmd.SetComputeVectorArrayParam(Shader,"_SourceProjectionX96",projectionX);cmd.SetComputeVectorArrayParam(Shader,"_SourceProjectionY96",projectionY);cmd.SetComputeIntParams(Shader,"_SourceEyeIds96",eyeIds);cmd.DispatchCompute(Shader,k,(width/4+7)/8,(height/4+7)/8,1);cmd.EndSample("WholeEye107 BVH visibility and joint selection");Frames++;
 }

 }
 public void ExternalBindings24(CommandBuffer cmd,ComputeShader cs,int k){
  Check();if(alphaSources==null||alphaSources.Any(t=>!t))throw new InvalidOperationException("24 borrowed alpha source invalidated");void Bind(string n,GraphicsBuffer b)=>cmd.SetComputeBufferParam(cs,k,n,b);
  Bind("_Triangles96",triangles);Bind("_Materials96",materials);Bind("_World96",World);Bind("_UV96",uv);Bind("_PartTriangles96",parts);Bind("_Nodes96",nodes);Bind("_Boxes96",boxes);

 }
 public void CheckMatrices24(Matrix4x4 view,Matrix4x4 projection){for(int i=0;i<16;i++){global::Execution24.DispatchBounds24.Finite(view[i]);global::Execution24.DispatchBounds24.Finite(projection[i]);}}
 public void CheckProjection24(Matrix4x4 view,Matrix4x4 projection){Check();if(alphaSources==null||alphaSources.Any(t=>!t))throw new InvalidOperationException("24 borrowed alpha source lost before submission");foreach(var m in cpuMaterials107)if(m.atlas.w!=0){int slot=(int)m.atlas.x;if(slot<0||slot>=alphaSources.Length||alphaSources[slot].width!=(int)m.atlas.y||alphaSources[slot].height!=(int)m.atlas.z)throw new InvalidOperationException("24 alpha sampler descriptor changed; explicit rebuild required");}CheckMatrices24(view,projection);foreach(var p in cpuWorld107){var v=view*p;var c=projection*v;if(c.w==0)throw new ArgumentException("24 undefined projected vertex");global::Execution24.DispatchBounds24.Finite((c.x/c.w*.5f+.5f)*480);global::Execution24.DispatchBounds24.Finite((.5f-c.y/c.w*.5f)*272);global::Execution24.DispatchBounds24.Finite(v.z);}}

// Current evaluated native colour ribbon. Bounded binary input, no large JSON.
public sealed class SourcePigment113:IDisposable{
// Members of SourcePigment113. The same authored-to-head projective mapping is
// used for the ring and its already validated upper pigment witnesses.
readonly List<Vector3> restHead159 = new List<Vector3>();
public Vector4 Ring159 { get; private set; } // current widths, current/rest heights
public Vector4 Centres159 { get; private set; } public Vector4 Physical160 {get;private set;}public Vector2 RowDirection161 {get;private set;} public Vector2 Depth160 {get;private set;}
void Measure159(Matrix4x4 model, Matrix4x4 toHead, Vector2 anchor, Vector4 bounds)
{
    if(restHead159.Count==0) mesh.GetUVs(1,restHead159);
    if(restHead159.Count!=mesh.vertexCount) throw new InvalidOperationException("159 missing bind-pose head coordinates");
    var measures=Vector4.zero; var centres=Vector4.zero;var physical160=Vector4.zero;var depth160=Vector2.zero;var direction161=Vector2.zero;
    for(int i=0;i<2;i++) {
        int slot=Array.FindIndex(eye.centres,c=>Mathf.RoundToInt(c.w)==ids[i]);
        if(slot<0) throw new InvalidOperationException("159 ring owner unavailable");
        var inv=view.eyeInverse[slot]; inv.m33=1;
        if(Mathf.Abs(inv.determinant)<1e-12f) throw new InvalidOperationException("159 singular registration");
        var transform=inv.inverse;
        Vector2 Project(Vector3 position) {
            Vector4 p=new Vector4(position.x,position.y,position.z,1);
            var v=transform*new Vector4(Vector4.Dot(p,px[i]),Vector4.Dot(p,py[i]),1,0);
            if(Mathf.Abs(v.z)<1e-12f) throw new InvalidOperationException("159 projected ring at infinity");
            return new Vector2(v.x/v.z-anchor.x-bounds.x,v.y/v.z-anchor.y-bounds.y);
        }
        var centreHead=eye.modelEyes[slot].centre;var ca=new Vector4(centreHead.x,centreHead.y,centreHead.z,1);
        var uv=new Vector4(Vector4.Dot(ca,px[i]),Vector4.Dot(ca,py[i]),1,0);var q0=transform*uv;uv.y+=1;var q1=transform*uv;
        if(Mathf.Abs(q0.z)<1e-12f||Mathf.Abs(q1.z)<1e-12f)throw new InvalidOperationException("161 invalid projected pigment direction");
        direction161[i]=q1.y/q1.z-q0.y/q0.z;
        var lo=new Vector2(float.PositiveInfinity,float.PositiveInfinity);var hi=-lo;
        float lowRest=float.PositiveInfinity, highRest=float.NegativeInfinity;
        var min160=new Vector2(float.PositiveInfinity,float.PositiveInfinity);var max160=-min160;
        float restMin160=float.PositiveInfinity,restMax160=float.NegativeInfinity,sumZ160=0;
        foreach(int index in ring24[i]) {
            var world160=model.MultiplyPoint3x4(vertices[index]); var xy160=view.Project(world160);
            float restY160=view.Project(eye.headMatrix.MultiplyPoint3x4(restHead159[index])).y;
            min160=Vector2.Min(min160,xy160);max160=Vector2.Max(max160,xy160);
            sumZ160-=camera.worldToCameraMatrix.MultiplyPoint3x4(world160).z;
            restMin160=Mathf.Min(restMin160,restY160);restMax160=Mathf.Max(restMax160,restY160);
            var current=Project(toHead.MultiplyPoint3x4(model.MultiplyPoint3x4(vertices[index])));
            float restY=Project(restHead159[index]).y;
            lo=Vector2.Min(lo,current);hi=Vector2.Max(hi,current);
            lowRest=Mathf.Min(lowRest,restY);highRest=Mathf.Max(highRest,restY);
        }
        if(!(hi.x>lo.x && highRest>lowRest)) throw new InvalidOperationException("159 degenerate aperture");
        if(!(max160.x>min160.x && restMax160>restMin160))throw new InvalidOperationException("160 invalid physical ring");
        physical160[i]=max160.x-min160.x;physical160[i+2]=(max160.y-min160.y)/(restMax160-restMin160);
        depth160[i]=sumZ160/ring24[i].Length;measures[i]=hi.x-lo.x; measures[i+2]=(hi.y-lo.y)/(highRest-lowRest);
        centres[i*2]=(lo.x+hi.x)*.5f; centres[i*2+1]=(lo.y+hi.y)*.5f;
    }
    Ring159=measures; Centres159=centres; Physical160=physical160; Depth160=depth160;RowDirection161=direction161;
}

 public struct Key{public Vector4 id,rgba,tag;}public readonly GraphicsBuffer keys,own,shared26,cells,tags,frame,points,groups,best,subsets,exposure,columns24,columnNative26,depth24,visibility24;readonly List<GraphicsBuffer> buffers=new List<GraphicsBuffer>();readonly EyeFeature348 eye;public Func<RectInt> OutputROI;RectInt R=>OutputROI==null?eye.roi:OutputROI();readonly Vector4[] palette;readonly string topology;public bool Disposed{get;private set;}public int DrawnFrame{get;private set;}=-1;
 public IEnumerable<GraphicsBuffer> Buffers=>buffers;public long OwnedBytes=>buffers.Sum(b=>(long)b.count*b.stride);
 GraphicsBuffer B(int n,int stride){var b=new GraphicsBuffer(GraphicsBuffer.Target.Structured,n,stride);buffers.Add(b);return b;}
 static float F(Json58 v)=>(float)(double)v;
// Inserted inside the isolated RegionDriver113, before the GPU key upload.
// SourceKey113 matches id and tag; only rgba is a display colour.
readonly Key[] sharedKeys158;Vector4 inkLow161,inkHigh161,inkSingle161;public bool Ink161;
bool shared158;
public void UseShared158()
{
    if (shared158) return;
    if (Disposed) throw new ObjectDisposedException(nameof(RegionDriver113));
    keys.SetData(sharedKeys158);
    shared158 = true;
}

static Key[] SharedEyeKeys158(Key[] source)
{
    // An iris row carries the authored vertical tone change. Match row rank in
    // each registered bank, independent of screen handedness and pose.
    // Sclera is one pigment family; separate source components retain their IDs.
    var display = (Key[])source.Clone();
    var ids = source.Select(k => (int)k.id.z).Distinct().OrderBy(i => i).ToArray();
    if (ids.Length != 2) throw new InvalidOperationException("Two registered eyes required");
    var rows = ids.ToDictionary(id => id, id => source.Where(k => (int)k.id.z == id && (int)k.id.w == 1)
        .Select(k => (int)k.id.y).Distinct().OrderBy(y => y).ToArray());
    if (rows[ids[0]].Length == 0 || rows[ids[0]].Length != rows[ids[1]].Length)
        throw new InvalidOperationException("Iris bank row correspondence unavailable");
    var labs = source.Select(k => SourcePalette58.LabRGB(new[]{(int)k.tag.y,(int)k.tag.z,(int)k.tag.w})).ToArray();
    var groups = Enumerable.Range(0,source.Length).GroupBy(i => {
        var key = source[i];
        if (key.id.w == 4) return -1;
        if (key.id.w != 1) throw new InvalidOperationException("Unexpected authored eye role");
        return Array.IndexOf(rows[(int)key.id.z], (int)key.id.y);
    });
    foreach (var group in groups)
    {
        var indices = group.ToArray();
        var count = indices.GroupBy(i => (int)source[i].id.z).ToDictionary(g => g.Key,g => g.Count());
        if (count.Count != 2) throw new InvalidOperationException("Missing homologous eye samples");
        // Equal votes per eye. Return an observed source key, with an RGB tie
        // break independent of eye owner, key ordering and screen orientation.
        double Cost(int i) {
            double sum = 0;
            foreach (int j in indices)
                for (int channel = 0; channel < 3; channel++) {
                    double d = labs[i][channel] - labs[j][channel];
                    sum += d*d / count[(int)source[j].id.z];
                }
            return sum;
        }
        int reference = indices.OrderBy(Cost).ThenBy(i=>source[i].tag.y)
            .ThenBy(i=>source[i].tag.z).ThenBy(i=>source[i].tag.w).First();
        foreach (int i in indices) display[i].rgba = source[reference].rgba;
    }
    return display;
}

 public void Record(RenderGraph graph,UniversalResourceData resources,Vector2 origin,int width,int height){

  // after GPU RT flip, raw texture row0 maps to the ROI's maximum Project Y.
  var crop=Matrix4x4.identity;crop.m00=480f/w;crop.m03=(480f-2*origin.x-w)/w;crop.m11=272f/h;crop.m13=(2*origin.y+h-272f)/h;
  var vp=GL.GetGPUProjectionMatrix(crop*full.effectiveProjection,true)*cam.worldToCameraMatrix;GPUVP=vp;
  foreach(var mat in materials){
   mat.SetMatrix("_HeadHairGPUVP25",vp);mat.SetVector("_ROI348",new Vector4(origin.x,272-origin.y-h,w,h));
   mat.SetMatrix("_WorldToHead348",eye.headMatrix.inverse);mat.SetInt("_SupportKind348",4);
   mat.SetVector("_ClipShift333",Vector4.zero);mat.SetVector("_Profile333",full.motion.animation.shadeProfile);
   mat.SetVector("_SurfaceInvRaster335",new Vector4(1f/480,1f/272,0,0));mat.SetFloat("_SurfaceOwnShade335",full.motion.animation.shadeProfile.x);
   mat.SetFloat("_ShadeLayer335",full.shadeLayer?1:0);mat.SetFloat("_SurfaceShadeSteps335",full.surfaceShadeSteps);
  }
  using(var b=graph.AddRasterRenderPass<Data>("25 original S4 hair on continuous head grid",out var d)){
   d.owner=this;d.model=full.motion.surface.outputs[2].transform.localToWorldMatrix;
   for(int i=0;i<5;i++)b.SetRenderAttachment(graph.ImportTexture(Fields[i]),i,AccessFlags.Write);
   b.SetRenderAttachmentDepth(graph.ImportTexture(depth),AccessFlags.Write);b.UseAllGlobalTextures(true);
   if(resources.mainShadowsTexture.IsValid())b.UseTexture(resources.mainShadowsTexture);
   if(resources.additionalShadowsTexture.IsValid())b.UseTexture(resources.additionalShadowsTexture);
   b.AllowPassCulling(false);b.SetRenderFunc(static(Data data,RasterGraphContext context)=>data.owner.Draw(data,context));
  }
  RecordedFrame=Time.frameCount;
 }
 void Draw(Data data,RasterGraphContext context){
  if(Disposed||RecordedFrame!=Time.frameCount)throw new InvalidOperationException("25 stale head hair pass");
  context.cmd.ClearRenderTarget(RTClearFlags.All,Color.clear,1,0);
  if(Hair154)for(int i=0;i<4;i++)context.cmd.DrawMesh(mesh,data.model,materials[i],i,0);
  RenderedFrame=Time.frameCount;
 }
 public void Bind(CommandBuffer cmd,ComputeShader shader,int kernel,Vector2 expectedOrigin){
  if(Disposed||RenderedFrame!=Time.frameCount||Origin!=expectedOrigin)throw new InvalidOperationException("25 current hair/eye footprint mismatch");
  cmd.SetComputeTextureParam(shader,kernel,"_HeadHairRGBA25",Fields[0].rt);cmd.SetComputeTextureParam(shader,kernel,"_HeadHairMeta25",Fields[1].rt);
  cmd.SetComputeIntParams(shader,"_HeadHairSize25",Width,Height);
 }
 public void AssertRendered(){if(Disposed||RenderedFrame!=Time.frameCount)throw new InvalidOperationException("25 head hair not rendered in current frame");}
 void ReleaseTargets(){if(Fields!=null)foreach(var f in Fields)f?.Release();depth?.Release();Fields=null;depth=null;Width=Height=0;}
 public void Dispose(){if(Disposed)return;Disposed=true;ReleaseTargets();foreach(var m in materials)if(m)UnityEngine.Object.DestroyImmediate(m);}
}


// Candidate-only output domain. Never changes EyeFeature348.roi/product77.
public static class OutputSupport26 {
 public static RectInt Union(RectInt original,Vector2 origin,int fineWidth,int fineHeight){
  if(!float.IsFinite(origin.x)||!float.IsFinite(origin.y)||fineWidth<=0||fineHeight<=0||fineWidth%4!=0||fineHeight%4!=0)
   throw new InvalidOperationException("26 finite canonical source grid required");
  int x=Mathf.CeilToInt(origin.x-.5f),y=Mathf.CeilToInt(origin.y-.5f);
  int x0=Mathf.Clamp(Math.Min(original.xMin,x),0,480),y0=Mathf.Clamp(Math.Min(original.yMin,y),0,272);
  int x1=Mathf.Clamp(Math.Max(original.xMax,x+fineWidth/4),0,480),y1=Mathf.Clamp(Math.Max(original.yMax,y+fineHeight/4),0,272);
  var r=new RectInt(x0,y0,x1-x0,y1-y0);
  if(r.width<=0||r.height<=0||(long)r.width*r.height>512)throw new InvalidOperationException("26 candidate output exceeds existing512-cell budget");
  return r;
 }
 public static Matrix4x4 Crop(RectInt roi,Matrix4x4 physical){
  if(roi.width<=0||roi.height<=0)throw new InvalidOperationException("26 empty output crop");
  var crop=Matrix4x4.identity;crop.m00=480f/roi.width;crop.m03=(480f-2*roi.x-roi.width)/roi.width;
  crop.m11=272f/roi.height;crop.m13=(2*roi.y+roi.height-272f)/roi.height;
  return crop*physical;
 }
}

public sealed class MotionClose106:IDisposable{
 readonly NativePlayer395 native;readonly string directory;PlayerMovement movement;Animator animator;SkinnedMeshRenderer source;UnityEngine.Mesh expected;int morph=-1,index,lastSerial=-1,warm=3;float oldSpeed,oldAnimatorSpeed,oldCapture,oldClose,requested;bool started,disposed;Action oldHook;InputSettings settings;string settingsJSON;float evaluatedError;int waits;

 public Arr58 warmup=new Arr58(),dynamicInput=new Arr58();

 public MotionClose106(NativePlayer395 n,string folder){native=n;directory=folder;}

 public void Start(){if(started)throw new InvalidOperationException("95 already started");movement=native.player.GetComponent<PlayerMovement>();animator=native.host.main.motion.animation.target;source=native.host.main.motion.surface.source[1];morph=source.sharedMesh.GetBlendShapeIndex("Fcl_EYE_Close");if(!movement||!animator||morph<0)throw new InvalidOperationException("Supported Close source missing");settings=InputSystem.settings;if(!settings)throw new InvalidOperationException("InputSettings missing");settingsJSON=JsonUtility.ToJson(settings);oldSpeed=movement.speed;oldAnimatorSpeed=animator.speed;oldCapture=Time.captureDeltaTime;oldClose=source.GetBlendShapeWeight(morph);oldHook=native.host.afterPose;CreateOnly40.Text(Path.Combine(directory,"STIMULUS_BEFORE95.json"),new Obj58{["settingsId"]=settings.GetEntityId().ToString(),["settingsJSON"]=settingsJSON,["movementSpeed"]=oldSpeed,["animatorSpeed"]=oldAnimatorSpeed,["captureDeltaTime"]=oldCapture,["Close"]=oldClose,["oldHookPresent"]=oldHook!=null,["expectedBakeMeshOwnership"]="new DontSave mesh, not source asset"}.ToString());expected=new UnityEngine.Mesh{name="95 owned state reference",hideFlags=HideFlags.DontSave};started=true;movement.speed=0;animator.speed=0;Time.captureDeltaTime=1f/60;requested=Values[0];native.host.afterPose+=Apply;}
 public bool Ready(int serial){if(serial==lastSerial)return false;lastSerial=serial;if(warm>0){warm--;return false;}if(source.GetBlendShapeWeight(morph)!=requested)return false;source.BakeMesh(expected,true);var filter=native.host.head.motion.surface.outputs[1];var map=filter.transform.worldToLocalMatrix*source.localToWorldMatrix;var want=expected.vertices;var actual=filter.sharedMesh.vertices;var sampled=native.host.headEyes.sampleFace.vertices;if(actual.Length!=want.Length||sampled.Length!=want.Length)throw new InvalidOperationException("95 evaluated topology mismatch");evaluatedError=0;for(int i=0;i<want.Length;i++){var p=map.MultiplyPoint3x4(want[i]);evaluatedError=Mathf.Max(evaluatedError,(p-actual[i]).magnitude,(actual[i]-sampled[i]).magnitude);}warmup.Add(new Obj58{["frame"]=Time.frameCount,["serial"]=serial,["stage"]=index,["requestedClose"]=requested,["evaluatedVsOwnedBakeMax"]=evaluatedError});if(evaluatedError>2e-6f){if(++waits>12)throw new InvalidOperationException("95 evaluated mesh did not reach requested source state; no timeout extension");return false;}waits=0;return true;}
 public Obj58 Snapshot(){if(InputSystem.settings!=settings||JsonUtility.ToJson(settings)!=settingsJSON)throw new InvalidOperationException("95 input changed");return new Obj58{["stage"]=Stage,["index"]=index,["requestedClose"]=requested,["getterClose"]=source.GetBlendShapeWeight(morph),["evaluatedVsOwnedBakeMax"]=evaluatedError,["evaluatedStateConditionPassed"]=evaluatedError<=2e-6f,["actualInput"]=new Arr58(GameInputBindings.ReadMovement().x,GameInputBindings.ReadMovement().y),["headJoinOffset"]=new Arr58(native.host.offset.x,native.host.offset.y),["deliveredDelta"]=new Arr58(native.deliveredDelta.x,native.deliveredDelta.y),["inputSettingsWritten"]=false};}
 public bool Advance(){index++;if(index>=Values.Length)return false;requested=Values[index];return true;}
 public void Dispose(){if(disposed)return;disposed=true;var errors=new Arr58();void Try(Action a){try{a();}catch(Exception e){errors.Add(e.ToString());}}if(started){Try(()=>{if(!native||!native.host)throw new InvalidOperationException("95 hook owner disappeared");native.host.afterPose-=Apply;if(native.host.afterPose!=oldHook)throw new InvalidOperationException("95 old hook differs");});Try(()=>{if(!source)throw new InvalidOperationException("95 source disappeared");source.SetBlendShapeWeight(morph,oldClose);});Try(()=>{if(movement)movement.speed=oldSpeed;});Try(()=>{if(animator)animator.speed=oldAnimatorSpeed;});Try(()=>Time.captureDeltaTime=oldCapture);}if(expected)UnityEngine.Object.DestroyImmediate(expected);bool input=!started||(InputSystem.settings==settings&&settings&&JsonUtility.ToJson(settings)==settingsJSON);Restored=errors.Count==0&&input&&(!started||(source&&source.GetBlendShapeWeight(morph)==oldClose&&movement&&movement.speed==oldSpeed&&animator&&animator.speed==oldAnimatorSpeed&&Time.captureDeltaTime==oldCapture));RestoreReport=new Obj58{["allRestored"]=Restored,["inputUnchanged"]=input,["inputSettingWrites"]=0,["devicesAdded"]=0,["ownedReferenceMeshDisposed"]=!expected,["errors"]=errors};}
}




// Observer stimuli only. Algorithm receives no Close value or test-stage flag.

namespace Execution24 {
public static class DispatchBounds24 {
 public static int Product(int width,int height,int multiple,int maximum){
  if(width<1||height<1||multiple<1||width%multiple!=0||height%multiple!=0||(long)width*height>maximum)throw new System.ArgumentException("24 grid/product bound");
  return width*height;
 }
 public static void Grid(int tw,int th,int sw,int sh,int cw,int ch,int rw,int rh,int keys,int curves){
  Product(tw,th,4,32768);Product(sw,sh,8,32768);Product(cw,ch,8,32768);Product(rw,rh,1,512);
  Product(sw/8,sh/8,1,512);
  if(tw!=sw/2||th!=sh/2||cw!=sw||ch!=sh||keys<1||keys>32||curves<1||curves>1024)throw new System.ArgumentException("24 source/carrier/layout contract");
 }
 public static void Topology(int vertices,int triangles,int materials,int primitiveStride,int partCount,int alphaCount){
  if(vertices<1||vertices>131072||triangles<1||triangles>262144||materials<1||materials>System.Int32.MaxValue/48||primitiveStride<1||(long)primitiveStride*4!=partCount||alphaCount<0||alphaCount>16777216)throw new System.ArgumentException("24 topology/resource bound");
 }
 public static void Triangle(int a,int b,int c,int material,int vertices,int materials){
  if(a<0||a>=vertices||b<0||b>=vertices||c<0||c>=vertices||material<0||material>=materials)throw new System.ArgumentException("24 triangle index");
 }
 public static void PartIndex(int index,int triangles){if(index< -1||index>=triangles)throw new System.ArgumentException("24 primitive map index");}
 public static void Finite(float value){if(System.Single.IsNaN(value)||System.Single.IsInfinity(value))throw new System.ArgumentException("24 non-finite geometry/matrix");}
 public static void Texture(float slot,float width,float height,float mode){
  Finite(slot);Finite(width);Finite(height);Finite(mode);
  if(mode!=0&&(slot<0||slot>=32||slot!=(int)slot||width<1||height<1||width!=(int)width||height!=(int)height))throw new System.ArgumentException("24 alpha texture descriptor");
 }
}

    }
   }else AllocateTargets();

 }
  RectInt? nativeCrop154;
  public void AllocateTargets(RectInt? nativeCrop = null){
  if(Disposed||fields!=null||nonHairMeta!=null)throw new InvalidOperationException("154 targets cannot be allocated twice");
   if(nativeCrop.HasValue){
    var c=nativeCrop.Value;
    if(SystemInfo.graphicsDeviceType!=GraphicsDeviceType.Direct3D11 ||
        c.x<0||c.y<0||c.width<1||c.height<1||c.xMax>960||c.yMax>544||
        (long)c.width*c.height>480L*272)
     throw new InvalidOperationException("154 native character crop is outside the D3D11 allocation budget");
   }
   nativeCrop154=nativeCrop;
   var rasterSize=nativeCrop?.size??new Vector2Int(480,272);
   try{
    nonHairMeta=Target("154 owned same-ray non-hair depth and binding");
   for(int i=0;i<materials.Length;i++)if(source.bindings[i].renderer==2)materials[i].SetTexture("_UnderHair154",nonHairMeta);
   fields=new RTHandle[lightTrace?7:4];output=new RTHandle[2];
