  }catch{Dispose();throw;}
 }
  RTHandle Target(string name){
   var size=nativeCrop154?.size??new Vector2Int(480,272);
   return RTHandles.Alloc(size.x,size.y,colorFormat:GraphicsFormat.R32G32B32A32_SFloat,filterMode:FilterMode.Point,name:name);
  }

  Matrix4x4 CropNativeCharacter154(Matrix4x4 fullGpuVP,RectInt region){
   // Use the evaluated, pre-quantization vertices. A crop that could cut
   // a surface may not be submitted even if last frame's silhouette fitted.
   foreach(var surface in eye.full.motion.surface.outputs){
    if(!surface||!surface.sharedMesh)throw new InvalidOperationException("154 native input surface is missing");
    var objectToClip=fullGpuVP*surface.transform.localToWorldMatrix;
    var bounds=surface.sharedMesh.bounds;
    for(int corner=0;corner<8;corner++){
     var v=bounds.center+Vector3.Scale(bounds.extents,new Vector3(
      (corner&1)==0?-1:1,(corner&2)==0?-1:1,(corner&4)==0?-1:1));
     var clip=objectToClip*new Vector4(v.x,v.y,v.z,1);
     if(!(clip.w>0)||!float.IsFinite(clip.w))
      throw new InvalidOperationException("154 native input has no valid conservative projection");
     float x=(1+clip.x/clip.w)*480, y=(1-clip.y/clip.w)*272;
     if(!float.IsFinite(x)||!float.IsFinite(y)||x<region.x+1||x>=region.xMax-1||y<region.y+1||y>=region.yMax-1)
      throw new InvalidOperationException("154 native input crop would cut a character surface");
    }
   }
   var crop=Matrix4x4.identity;
   crop.m00=960f/region.width;
   crop.m03=(960f-2*region.x-region.width)/region.width;
   crop.m11=544f/region.height;
   crop.m13=(2*region.y+region.height-544f)/region.height;
   return crop*fullGpuVP;
  }
 public void BindDirectionalShadows(RTHandle world,RTHandle self,Matrix4x4 worldToShadow,Matrix4x4 selfToShadow){
  if(Disposed||fields.Length!=7||!materials[0].shader.keywordSpace.FindKeyword("_T2_SPLIT_DIRECTIONAL154").isValid)
   throw new InvalidOperationException("154 isolated directional shadow variant is unavailable");
  int dimension=world.rt.width;
  if(dimension!=world.rt.height||dimension!=self.rt.width||dimension!=self.rt.height)
   throw new InvalidOperationException("154 directional map dimensions differ");
  directionalWorld=world;directionalSelf=self;
  foreach(var material in materials){
   material.EnableKeyword("_T2_SPLIT_DIRECTIONAL154");
   material.SetTexture("_DirectionalWorld154",world);material.SetTexture("_DirectionalSelf154",self);
   material.SetFloat("_DirectionalMapSize154",dimension);
   material.SetMatrix("_WorldToDirectional154",worldToShadow);material.SetMatrix("_SelfToDirectional154",selfToShadow);
  }
 }
 static PartGroup SourceGroup(int role)=>role switch {
  0 => PartGroup.Unassigned, // Mouth has no approved group in this node.
  1 or 2 or 4 or 6 => PartGroup.Eye,
  3 => PartGroup.FaceSkin,
  5 => PartGroup.Eyebrow,
  7 => PartGroup.BodySkin, // Shader uses the original per-UV stocking membership.
  8 => PartGroup.Bottom,
  9 => PartGroup.Shoes,
  10 => PartGroup.Top,
  11 or 12 or 13 or 14 => PartGroup.SourceHair,
  _ => throw new InvalidOperationException("154 source material role has no part-group binding")
 };
 public Matrix4x4 RasterVP {get;private set;}
 void BindStock(Material material,Full319View335 main,int levels){
  material.SetTexture("_StockPartMember154",main.stockMember);
  if(!useStockRamp||!material.HasProperty("_StockRampReady154"))return;
  if(levels==boundStockLevels&&main.stockShade.Equals(boundStockShade)&&main.stockLit.Equals(boundStockLit))return;
  if(levels<2||levels>stockRamp.Length||source.RegionCount>=71)throw new InvalidOperationException("154 stocking LUT address conflict");
  var lit=main.stockLit;var shade=main.stockShade;
  for(int i=0;i<3;i++)if(!float.IsFinite(lit[i])||!float.IsFinite(shade[i])||shade[i]<0||lit[i]<shade[i])
   throw new InvalidOperationException("154 invalid authored stocking endpoints");
  if(lit.x==shade.x&&lit.y==shade.y&&lit.z==shade.z)throw new InvalidOperationException("154 stocking endpoints do not distinguish shade");
  for(int q=0;q<stockRamp.Length;q++){
   int last=levels-1;
   stockRamp[q]=q==0?shade:q>=last?lit:Vector4.LerpUnclamped(shade,lit,q/(float)last);
  }
  material.SetVectorArray("_StockRamp154",stockRamp);material.SetInt("_StockRampReady154",1);
  boundStockLit=lit;boundStockShade=shade;boundStockLevels=levels;
 }
 Matrix4x4 CanvasDepthProjection(Matrix4x4 view,Camera camera){
  // The canvas contains only the character. Keep hardware Z within the evaluated
  // character bounds; world/occluder comparisons still use the unmodified view depth.
  var surfaces=eye.full.motion.surface.outputs;
  float min=float.PositiveInfinity,max=float.NegativeInfinity;
  foreach(var surface in surfaces){
   var bounds=surface.sharedMesh.bounds;
   var localToView=view*surface.transform.localToWorldMatrix;
   float centre=localToView.MultiplyPoint3x4(bounds.center).z;
   var extents=bounds.extents;
   float radius=Mathf.Abs(localToView.m20)*extents.x+Mathf.Abs(localToView.m21)*extents.y+Mathf.Abs(localToView.m22)*extents.z;
   min=Mathf.Min(min,centre-radius);max=Mathf.Max(max,centre+radius);
  }
  float span=max-min;
  if(!float.IsFinite(span)||span<=0)throw new InvalidOperationException("154 evaluated depth bounds are empty");
  if(max>=-camera.nearClipPlane||min<=-camera.farClipPlane)return RasterVP;
  // Include rounding slack around the conservative box, with no depth clip at a box face.
  float centreZ=min+span*.5f;
  float scale=(SystemInfo.usesReversedZBuffer?1f:-1f)/(span*1.5f);
  var depthVP=RasterVP;
  depthVP.SetRow(2,view.GetRow(2)*scale+new Vector4(0,0,0,.5f-centreZ*scale));
  return depthVP;
 }
 public void Record(RenderGraph graph,UniversalResourceData res,bool hairOnlyEvidence=false){if(Disposed)throw new ObjectDisposedException(nameof(IntegratedCanvas154));double start=EditorApplication.timeSinceStartup;var main=NativePlayer395.Active.host.main;var mainCamera=main.motion.camera;var mainView=mainCamera.worldToCameraMatrix;
  if(main.motion.mode!=2||!main.motion.actorLocalGrid||!mainCamera.orthographic||!main.stockMember)
   throw new InvalidOperationException("154 requires the actor-local orthographic grid and source stocking membership");
  // effectiveProjection already has the inverse root phase from MotionView331.
  // Submit body and head to that same clip matrix, without a second head pivot
  // or rounded join. This canvas uses the same evaluated surface for all parts.
  RasterVP=GL.GetGPUProjectionMatrix(main.effectiveProjection,true)*mainView;
   float rasterScale=1f;
   var rasterROI=new Vector4(0,0,480,272);
   if(nativeCrop154.HasValue){
    var region=nativeCrop154.Value;
    if(ObservationWindow.HasValue)throw new InvalidOperationException("154 low-resolution scissor cannot be applied to the native crop");
    RasterVP=CropNativeCharacter154(RasterVP,region);
    rasterScale=2f;
    // The lighting helpers expect the same 480-space screen UV and derivative scale.
    rasterROI=new Vector4(region.x*.5f,region.y*.5f,region.width*.5f,region.height*.5f);
   }
   var depthVP=CanvasDepthProjection(mainView,mainCamera);
  foreach(var b in source.bindings){var m=materials[b.ordinal];source.Bind(m,b.renderer,b.submesh);m.SetMatrix("_ProbeGPUVP91",RasterVP);m.SetMatrix("_DepthGPU154",depthVP);m.SetMatrix("_View154",mainView);m.SetVector("_Shift154",Vector4.zero);if(b.material==7)BindStock(m,main,b.levels);m.SetFloat("_RasterScale91",rasterScale);m.SetVector("_ROI348",rasterROI);m.SetVector("_SurfaceInvRaster335",new Vector4(1f/480,1f/272,0,0));m.SetFloat("_FaceShadeSteps335",main.faceShadeSteps);m.SetFloat("_SurfaceShadeSteps335",main.surfaceShadeSteps);m.SetInt("_SurfaceMaskReady335",0);m.SetFloat("_BindSH91",0);}
  RecordSurfaces(graph,res,hair:false,clear:true,complete:!Hair,skipSurfaces:hairOnlyEvidence);
  if(Hair){
   // Copy the exact shader-produced z/binding texel before the shared depth is
   // continued. A texture cannot be sampled while it is also a colour attachment.
   using(var copy=graph.AddUnsafePass<CopyData>("154 preserve same-ray non-hair metadata",out var d)){
    d.source=graph.ImportTexture(fields[1]);d.destination=graph.ImportTexture(nonHairMeta);
    copy.UseTexture(d.source,AccessFlags.Read);copy.UseTexture(d.destination,AccessFlags.Write);
    copy.SetRenderFunc(static(CopyData p,UnsafeGraphContext ctx)=>
     CommandBufferHelpers.GetNativeCommandBuffer(ctx.cmd).CopyTexture(p.source,p.destination));
   }
   RecordSurfaces(graph,res,hair:true,clear:false,complete:true,skipSurfaces:false);
  }
  RecordCPUms=(EditorApplication.timeSinceStartup-start)*1000;
 }
 void RecordSurfaces(RenderGraph graph,UniversalResourceData res,bool hair,bool clear,bool complete,bool skipSurfaces){
  using var pass=graph.AddRasterRenderPass<Data>(hair?"154 resolve hair against same-ray character depth":"154 original surface and finite SourcePalette58",out var d);
  d.owner=skipSurfaces?null:this;d.hair=hair;d.clear=clear;d.complete=complete;d.window=ObservationWindow;
  var access=clear?AccessFlags.WriteAll:AccessFlags.ReadWrite;
  for(int i=0;i<fields.Length;i++)pass.SetRenderAttachment(graph.ImportTexture(fields[i]),i,access);
  pass.SetRenderAttachmentDepth(graph.ImportTexture(depth),access);
  if(hair)pass.UseTexture(graph.ImportTexture(nonHairMeta),AccessFlags.Read);
  if(directionalWorld!=null){pass.UseTexture(graph.ImportTexture(directionalWorld),AccessFlags.Read);pass.UseTexture(graph.ImportTexture(directionalSelf),AccessFlags.Read);}
  pass.UseAllGlobalTextures(true);if(res.mainShadowsTexture.IsValid())pass.UseTexture(res.mainShadowsTexture);if(res.additionalShadowsTexture.IsValid())pass.UseTexture(res.additionalShadowsTexture);
  pass.AllowPassCulling(false);
  pass.SetRenderFunc(static(Data p,RasterGraphContext ctx)=>{
   if(p.clear)ctx.cmd.ClearRenderTarget(RTClearFlags.All,Color.clear,1,0);
   p.owner?.Draw(ctx,p.hair,p.complete,p.window);
  });
 }
 void Draw(RasterGraphContext ctx,bool hair,bool complete,Rect? window){
  if(window.HasValue)ctx.cmd.EnableScissorRect(window.Value);
  try{
   foreach(var b in source.bindings){
    if((b.renderer==2)!=hair)continue;
    var mf=eye.full.motion.surface.outputs[b.renderer];
    ctx.cmd.DrawMesh(mf.sharedMesh,mf.transform.localToWorldMatrix,materials[b.ordinal],b.submesh,0);
   }
   if(complete)RenderedFrame=Time.frameCount;
  }finally{if(window.HasValue)ctx.cmd.DisableScissorRect();}
 }
 public void Compose(RenderGraph graph,Forward113 forward){var h=NativePlayer395.Active.host;var roi=forward.OutputROI;compose.SetTexture("_Canvas154",fields[0]);compose.SetTexture("_Meta154",fields[1]);compose.SetTexture("_Source154",fields[2]);compose.SetTexture("_Light154",fields[3]);compose.SetTexture("_Eye154",forward.Fields[2]);compose.SetTexture("_EyeOwner154",forward.Fields[3]);compose.SetVector("_EyeROI154",new Vector4(roi.x,272-roi.y-roi.height,roi.width,roi.height));compose.SetVector("_Join154",new Vector4(h.offset.x,h.offset.y,h.depthOffset,0));
  using var pass=graph.AddRasterRenderPass<Data>("154 clean full canvas -> canonical joint eyes -> external hair",out var d);d.owner=this;d.forward=forward;foreach(var t in fields)pass.UseTexture(graph.ImportTexture(t),AccessFlags.Read);foreach(var t in forward.Fields)pass.UseTexture(graph.ImportTexture(t),AccessFlags.Read);for(int i=0;i<2;i++)pass.SetRenderAttachment(graph.ImportTexture(output[i]),i,AccessFlags.Write);pass.AllowPassCulling(false);pass.SetRenderFunc(static(Data d,RasterGraphContext ctx)=>{d.forward.AssertRendered();if(d.owner.RenderedFrame!=Time.frameCount)throw new InvalidOperationException("154 source/frame mismatch");ctx.cmd.DrawProcedural(Matrix4x4.identity,d.owner.compose,0,MeshTopology.Triangles,3);});
 }
 public void Dispose(){if(Disposed)return;Disposed=true;if(materials!=null)foreach(var m in materials)if(m)UnityEngine.Object.DestroyImmediate(m);if(compose)UnityEngine.Object.DestroyImmediate(compose);if(fields!=null)foreach(var t in fields)t?.Release();if(output!=null)foreach(var t in output)t?.Release();depth?.Release();nonHairMeta?.Release();fields=null;output=null;depth=null;nonHairMeta=null;}
}
}


namespace Unity.AI.Assistant.Agent.Dynamic.Extension.Editor {
public static class ShadowCropAxis154 {
    public static bool TryPlan(float centre, float minimum, float maximum,
        int tileSize, int size, int padding, out float worldStart, out float selfStart) {
        worldStart = selfStart = 0;
        if (!float.IsFinite(centre) || !float.IsFinite(minimum) || !float.IsFinite(maximum) ||
            minimum > maximum || padding < 0 || size <= 2L * padding || tileSize < size)
            return false;
        selfStart = centre - size * .5f;
        worldStart = Math.Clamp(MathF.Floor(selfStart), 0, tileSize - size);
        return Fits(worldStart) && Fits(selfStart);

        bool Fits(float start) => minimum - start >= padding && maximum - start < size - padding;
    }
}

// Each receipt belongs to one presentation and its final output use.
public sealed class ApertureTrialUse154 {
    enum State { NoUse, Awaiting, Confirmed, ReleaseUnverified, Released }
    readonly object receiptLock = new object();
    State state;
    int generation, receiptResult;
    bool receiptQueued;
    public bool LastUseConfirmed => state == State.Confirmed || state == State.Released;

    public void BeginPresent() {
        if (state != State.NoUse && state != State.Confirmed)
            throw new InvalidOperationException("154 preceding use is unsettled or the owner is closed");
        lock (receiptLock) {
            generation++;
            receiptQueued = false;
            receiptResult = 0;
        }
        state = State.Awaiting;
    }

    public void Confirm() {
        if (state == State.Confirmed) return;
        if (state != State.Awaiting)
            throw new InvalidOperationException("154 no pending presentation to confirm");
        state = State.Confirmed;
    }

    public bool QueueCompletion(Action<Action<bool>> request) {
        int expected;
        lock (receiptLock) {
            if (state != State.Awaiting || receiptQueued) return false;
            receiptQueued = true;
            expected = generation;
        }
        request(success => {
            lock (receiptLock)
                if (generation == expected && receiptResult == 0) receiptResult = success ? 1 : -1;
        });
        return true;
    }

    public bool ObserveQueuedCompletion() {
        lock (receiptLock)
            if (state == State.Awaiting && receiptResult == 1) Confirm();
        return LastUseConfirmed;
    }

    public bool TryConfirmPresentedRead(int serialBefore, int serialAfter, Action readOutput) {
        if (state != State.Awaiting || serialAfter != unchecked(serialBefore + 1))
            return false;
        readOutput();
        Confirm();
        return true;
    }

    public bool TryRelease(bool recordingAttached, Func<bool> releaseAndVerify) {
        ObserveQueuedCompletion();
        if (state == State.Released) return true;
        if (recordingAttached || (state != State.NoUse && state != State.Confirmed)) return false;
        // A partial or throwing disposal is never repeated.
        state = State.ReleaseUnverified;
        if (!releaseAndVerify()) return false;
        state = State.Released;
        return true;
    }
}
}

namespace Unity.AI.Assistant.Agent.Dynamic.Extension.Editor {
// Validation path only. Uses the camera's original directional cascade math,
// the native caster list for the world, and a root-relative atlas for the actor.
public sealed class SplitDirectionalShadow154:IDisposable {
 const int Padding=8;
 readonly int size;
 const float SelfDepthSpan=8;
 readonly NativePlayer395 native;
 readonly MeshFilter[] surfaces;
 readonly Renderer[] casters;
 readonly Material[][] originalMaterials;
 readonly Material[][] casterMaterials;
 readonly int[][] casterPasses;
 readonly RTHandle world,self;
 static readonly int WorldPass=Shader.PropertyToID("_T2WorldOnly154");
 static readonly int CasterOffset=Shader.PropertyToID("_T2CasterOffset154");
 static readonly int ShadowBias=Shader.PropertyToID("_ShadowBias");
 static readonly int LightDirection=Shader.PropertyToID("_LightDirection");
 static readonly int LightPosition=Shader.PropertyToID("_LightPosition");
 public Matrix4x4 WorldToShadow{get;private set;}
 public Matrix4x4 SelfToShadow{get;private set;}
 public RTHandle World=>world;
 public RTHandle Self=>self;
 public int Cascade{get;private set;}
 public int RecordedFrame{get;private set;}=-1;
 public int RecordedBatches{get;private set;}
 public int SelfDraws{get;private set;}
 public bool TraceCoverage{get;set;}
 public object LastCoverage{get;private set;}
 sealed class Data {
  public SplitDirectionalShadow154 owner;
  public bool self,oldPunctual;
  public RendererListHandle list;
  public float oldWorldPass;
  public Matrix4x4 view,projection,cameraView,cameraProjection;
  public Vector4 bias,direction,lightPosition,oldBias,oldDirection,oldPosition;
 }
 public SplitDirectionalShadow154(NativePlayer395 n,Shader body,Shader toon,int mapSize=256){
  native=n;
  if(mapSize!=256&&mapSize!=512&&mapSize!=1024)
   throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(nameof(mapSize));
  size=mapSize;
  surfaces=n.host.main.motion.surface.outputs;
  if(surfaces.Length!=3)throw new InvalidOperationException("154 directional test requires the three current actor meshes");
  casters=surfaces.Select(m=>m.GetComponent<Renderer>()).ToArray();
  originalMaterials=new Material[casters.Length][];
  casterMaterials=new Material[casters.Length][];
  casterPasses=new int[casters.Length][];
  if(Shader.GetGlobalFloat(WorldPass)!=0)throw new InvalidOperationException("154 exclusive world pass flag is busy");
  try{
   for(int i=0;i<casters.Length;i++){
    var caster=casters[i];
    if(!caster||!caster.enabled||caster.shadowCastingMode!=ShadowCastingMode.ShadowsOnly||caster.GetComponent<Collider>())
     throw new InvalidOperationException("154 actor caster identity changed");
    var original=originalMaterials[i]=caster.sharedMaterials;
    if(original.Length!=surfaces[i].sharedMesh.subMeshCount)throw new InvalidOperationException("154 caster slots changed");
    var owned=casterMaterials[i]=new Material[original.Length];
    casterPasses[i]=new int[original.Length];
    for(int j=0;j<original.Length;j++){
     var material=original[j];
     var shader=material.shader.name switch {
      "EValidation/CommonSurface329"=>body,
      "VRM10/Universal Render Pipeline/MToon10"=>toon,
      _=>throw new InvalidOperationException("154 unrecognized actor shadow caster shader")
     };
     owned[j]=new Material(material){shader=shader,name="154 owned directional caster "+i+"/"+j,hideFlags=HideFlags.HideAndDontSave};
     owned[j].shaderKeywords=material.shaderKeywords;
     owned[j].renderQueue=material.renderQueue;
     int pass=casterPasses[i][j]=owned[j].FindPass("ShadowCaster");
     if(pass<0||pass!=material.FindPass("ShadowCaster"))
      throw new InvalidOperationException("154 original ShadowCaster pass layout changed");
     ShaderUtil.CompilePass(owned[j],pass,true);
     if(ShaderUtil.ShaderHasError(shader))throw new InvalidOperationException("154 actor shadow variant failed");
     SelfDraws++;
    }
   }
   if(SelfDraws!=15)throw new InvalidOperationException("154 actor directional pass budget changed");
   world=RTHandles.Alloc(size,size,depthBufferBits:DepthBits.Depth16,filterMode:FilterMode.Bilinear,isShadowMap:true,name:"154 owned cropped directional world shadow");
   self=RTHandles.Alloc(size,size,depthBufferBits:DepthBits.Depth32,filterMode:FilterMode.Bilinear,isShadowMap:true,name:"154 owned cropped directional actor shadow");
  }catch{Dispose();throw;}
 }
 public void BindCasterMaterials(){
  for(int i=0;i<casters.Length;i++)
   if(!casters[i]||!casters[i].sharedMaterials.SequenceEqual(originalMaterials[i]))
    throw new InvalidOperationException("154 original caster materials changed before binding");
  for(int i=0;i<casters.Length;i++)casters[i].sharedMaterials=casterMaterials[i];
 }
 public void SetHairCasterOffset(Vector3 offset){
  foreach(var material in casterMaterials[2])material.SetVector(CasterOffset,offset);
 }
 Bounds ActorBounds(){
  var corners=new Vector3[8];Bounds total=default;bool first=true;
  foreach(var surface in surfaces){
   var b=surface.sharedMesh.bounds;
   Corners(b,corners);
   foreach(var local in corners){
    var worldPoint=surface.transform.TransformPoint(local);
    if(first){total=new Bounds(worldPoint,Vector3.zero);first=false;}else total.Encapsulate(worldPoint);
   }
  }
  return total;
 }
 static void Corners(Bounds b,Vector3[] points){
  for(int i=0;i<8;i++)points[i]=b.center+Vector3.Scale(b.extents,new Vector3((i&1)==0?-1:1,(i&2)==0?-1:1,(i&4)==0?-1:1));
 }
 Matrix4x4 Crop(Matrix4x4 original,Vector2 start,int tileSize){
  float scale=tileSize/(float)size;
  var w=original.GetRow(3);
  var p=original;
  p.SetRow(0,original.GetRow(0)*scale+w*(scale-1-2*start.x/size));
  p.SetRow(1,original.GetRow(1)*scale+w*(scale-1-2*start.y/size));
  return p;
 }
 static Matrix4x4 ShadowTransform(Matrix4x4 projection,Matrix4x4 view){
  if(SystemInfo.usesReversedZBuffer)projection.SetRow(2,-projection.GetRow(2));
  var bias=Matrix4x4.identity;
  bias.m00=bias.m11=bias.m22=bias.m03=bias.m13=bias.m23=.5f;
  return bias*projection*view;
 }
 public void Record(RenderGraph graph,ContextContainer frame){
  var shadows=frame.Get<UniversalShadowData>();
  var lighting=frame.Get<UniversalLightData>();
  var rendering=frame.Get<UniversalRenderingData>();
  var camera=frame.Get<UniversalCameraData>().camera;
  int lightIndex=lighting.mainLightIndex;
  if(camera!=native.localCamera||!shadows.supportsMainLightShadows||lightIndex<0)
   throw new InvalidOperationException("154 no original directional shadow data");
  var main=lighting.visibleLights[lightIndex];
  if(main.lightType!=LightType.Directional||!main.light||main.light.shadows==LightShadows.None)
   throw new InvalidOperationException("154 main light is not an actual directional caster");
  int tiles=shadows.mainLightShadowCascadesCount;
  int atlasWidth=shadows.mainLightShadowmapWidth,atlasHeight=shadows.mainLightShadowmapHeight;
  int tileSize=ShadowUtils.GetMaxTileResolutionInAtlas(atlasWidth,atlasHeight,tiles);
  if(tileSize<size||tiles<1||tiles>4)throw new InvalidOperationException("154 original shadow tile not supported");
  var cull=rendering.cullResults;
  var bounds=ActorBounds();var points=new Vector3[8];Corners(bounds,points);
  var tried=TraceCoverage?new List<object>():null;
  float[] XYZ(Vector3 v)=>new[]{v.x,v.y,v.z};
  if(TraceCoverage)LastCoverage=new {
   camera=camera.name,cameraPosition=XYZ(camera.transform.position),
   cameraRotation=new[]{camera.transform.rotation.x,camera.transform.rotation.y,camera.transform.rotation.z,camera.transform.rotation.w},
   camera.nearClipPlane,camera.farClipPlane,camera.orthographicSize,
   player=XYZ(native.player.position), actorMin=XYZ(bounds.min),actorMax=XYZ(bounds.max),
   cascadeCount=tiles,cascades=tried
  };
  ShadowSliceData slice=default;
  Cascade=-1;
  for(int i=0;i<tiles;i++){
   if(!ShadowUtils.ExtractDirectionalLightMatrix(ref cull,shadows,lightIndex,i,atlasWidth,
     tiles==2?atlasHeight/2:atlasHeight,tileSize,main.light.shadowNearPlane,out var sphere,out var next))
    throw new InvalidOperationException("154 original cascade matrix unavailable");
   float closest=bounds.SqrDistance(sphere),radiusSquared=sphere.w*sphere.w;
   if(tried!=null)tried.Add(new {index=i,center=XYZ(sphere),radius=sphere.w,closest,
       farthest=points.Max(q=>((Vector3)sphere-q).sqrMagnitude),radiusSquared});
   if(tiles==1||closest<radiusSquared){
    if(tiles>1&&points.Any(q=>((Vector3)sphere-q).sqrMagnitude>=sphere.w*sphere.w))
     throw new InvalidOperationException("154 actor crosses original cascade boundary; bounded proof uses one slice");
    Cascade=i;slice=next;break;
   }
  }
  if(Cascade<0)throw new InvalidOperationException("154 actor is outside the original shadow cascades");
  var view=slice.viewMatrix;var projection=slice.projectionMatrix;var clip=projection*view;
  Vector2 Pixel(Vector3 worldPoint){
   var q=clip.MultiplyPoint(worldPoint);return new Vector2((q.x*.5f+.5f)*tileSize,(q.y*.5f+.5f)*tileSize);
  }
  var root=native.player.position;
  // A constant vertical reference in the actor frame avoids pose-dependent grid drift.
  var anchor=root+Vector3.up*(native.bodyHeight*.5f);
  var centre=Pixel(anchor);
  var min=new Vector2(float.PositiveInfinity,float.PositiveInfinity);
  var max=new Vector2(float.NegativeInfinity,float.NegativeInfinity);
  foreach(var corner in points){
   var p=Pixel(corner);
   if(!float.IsFinite(p.x)||!float.IsFinite(p.y))
    throw new InvalidOperationException("154 non-finite shadow projection");
   min=Vector2.Min(min,p);max=Vector2.Max(max,p);
  }
  if(!ShadowCropAxis154.TryPlan(centre.x,min.x,max.x,tileSize,size,Padding,out float wx,out float sx)||
     !ShadowCropAxis154.TryPlan(centre.y,min.y,max.y,tileSize,size,Padding,out float wy,out float sy))
   throw new InvalidOperationException($"154 {size} shadow crop cannot contain the actor and PCF margin");
  var startWorld=new Vector2(wx,wy);
  var startSelf=new Vector2(sx,sy);
  var worldProj=Crop(projection,startWorld,tileSize);
  var selfProj=Crop(projection,startSelf,tileSize);
  float centreZ=view.MultiplyPoint(anchor).z;
  if(!float.IsFinite(centreZ)||points.Any(q=>{
   float z=view.MultiplyPoint(q).z;
   return !float.IsFinite(z)||Mathf.Abs(z-centreZ)>=SelfDepthSpan*.45f;
  }))
   throw new InvalidOperationException("154 actor exceeds bounded root shadow depth span");
  selfProj.SetRow(2,new Vector4(0,0,-2f/SelfDepthSpan,2f*centreZ/SelfDepthSpan));
  WorldToShadow=ShadowTransform(worldProj,view);SelfToShadow=ShadowTransform(selfProj,view);
  var settings=new ShadowDrawingSettings(cull,lightIndex){
   useRenderingLayerMaskTest=UniversalRenderPipeline.asset.useRenderingLayers,splitIndex=Cascade
  };
  RecordedFrame=Time.frameCount;
  RecordedBatches++;
  var worldList=graph.CreateShadowRendererList(ref settings);
  var bias=ShadowUtils.GetShadowBias(ref main,lightIndex,shadows,projection,tileSize);
  RecordAtlas(graph,camera,world,worldList,view,worldProj,main,bias,false);
  RecordAtlas(graph,camera,self,default,view,selfProj,main,bias,true);
 }
 void RecordAtlas(RenderGraph graph,Camera camera,RTHandle target,RendererListHandle list,Matrix4x4 view,Matrix4x4 projection,VisibleLight light,Vector4 bias,bool actor){
  using var pass=graph.AddRasterRenderPass<Data>(actor?"154 directional actor-only root crop":"154 directional world-only physical crop",out var d);
  d.owner=this;d.self=actor;d.list=list;d.oldWorldPass=Shader.GetGlobalFloat(WorldPass);d.view=view;d.projection=projection;
  d.cameraView=camera.worldToCameraMatrix;d.cameraProjection=camera.projectionMatrix;
  d.bias=bias;d.direction=-light.localToWorldMatrix.GetColumn(2);d.lightPosition=light.localToWorldMatrix.GetColumn(3);
  d.oldBias=Shader.GetGlobalVector(ShadowBias);d.oldDirection=Shader.GetGlobalVector(LightDirection);d.oldPosition=Shader.GetGlobalVector(LightPosition);
  d.oldPunctual=Shader.IsKeywordEnabled("_CASTING_PUNCTUAL_LIGHT_SHADOW");
  pass.SetRenderAttachmentDepth(graph.ImportTexture(target),AccessFlags.WriteAll);
  if(!actor)pass.UseRendererList(list);
  pass.AllowGlobalStateModification(true);pass.AllowPassCulling(false);
  pass.SetRenderFunc(static(Data p,RasterGraphContext ctx)=>{
   var cmd=ctx.cmd;
   cmd.ClearRenderTarget(RTClearFlags.Depth,Color.clear,1,0);
   cmd.SetViewport(new Rect(0,0,p.owner.size,p.owner.size));cmd.SetViewProjectionMatrices(p.view,p.projection);
   cmd.SetGlobalVector(ShadowBias,p.bias);cmd.SetGlobalVector(LightDirection,p.direction);cmd.SetGlobalVector(LightPosition,p.lightPosition);
   cmd.DisableShaderKeyword("_CASTING_PUNCTUAL_LIGHT_SHADOW");cmd.SetGlobalDepthBias(1,2.5f);
   cmd.SetGlobalFloat(WorldPass,p.self?0:1);
   try{
    if(p.self)p.owner.DrawActor(cmd);
    else cmd.DrawRendererList(p.list);
   }finally{
    cmd.SetGlobalFloat(WorldPass,p.oldWorldPass);
    cmd.SetGlobalDepthBias(0,0);cmd.SetGlobalVector(ShadowBias,p.oldBias);cmd.SetGlobalVector(LightDirection,p.oldDirection);cmd.SetGlobalVector(LightPosition,p.oldPosition);
    if(p.oldPunctual)cmd.EnableShaderKeyword("_CASTING_PUNCTUAL_LIGHT_SHADOW");
    cmd.SetViewProjectionMatrices(p.cameraView,p.cameraProjection);
   }
  });
 }
 void DrawActor(RasterCommandBuffer cmd){
  for(int i=0;i<surfaces.Length;i++)
   for(int j=0;j<casterPasses[i].Length;j++)
    cmd.DrawMesh(surfaces[i].sharedMesh,surfaces[i].transform.localToWorldMatrix,casterMaterials[i][j],j,casterPasses[i][j]);
 }
 public void Dispose(){
  world?.Release();self?.Release();
  for(int i=0;i<casters.Length;i++){
   if(casters[i]&&originalMaterials[i]!=null&&casterMaterials[i]!=null&&
      casters[i].sharedMaterials.SequenceEqual(casterMaterials[i]))casters[i].sharedMaterials=originalMaterials[i];
   if(casterMaterials[i]==null)continue;
   foreach(var material in casterMaterials[i])if(material)UnityEngine.Object.DestroyImmediate(material);
  }
 }
}
}

namespace Unity.AI.Assistant.Agent.Dynamic.Extension.Editor {
// The ordinary Gameplay397 camera and world passes still own rendering.
// A collider selects a route. Only the captured world depth can prove occlusion.
public sealed class WalkWorldStimulus154 {
    static readonly float[] WorldDistances = {.55f,1.70f,2.85f};
    static readonly float[] OpenDistances = {.14f,.32f,.50f};
    public readonly BoxCollider Blocker;
    public readonly Vector3 Start, Direction, CameraPosition;
    public readonly Quaternion Facing, CameraRotation;
    public readonly Vector2 Input;
    public readonly int[] PlannedEyeHits;
    public readonly float[] Distances;

    WalkWorldStimulus154(BoxCollider blocker, Vector3 start, Vector3 direction,
                        Vector3 cameraPosition, Quaternion cameraRotation, Vector2 input, int[] plannedEyeHits) {
        Blocker=blocker; Start=start; Direction=direction; CameraPosition=cameraPosition;
        CameraRotation=cameraRotation; Input=input; PlannedEyeHits=plannedEyeHits;
        Distances=blocker?WorldDistances:OpenDistances;
        Facing=Quaternion.LookRotation(direction,Vector3.up);
    }

    static Vector3 Horizontal(Vector3 v) => Vector3.ProjectOnPlane(v,Vector3.up).normalized;
    public static float[] XYZ(Vector3 v) => new[]{v.x,v.y,v.z};

    public static WalkWorldStimulus154 Plan(NativePlayer395 native, Camera camera, List<object> trials) {
        var pos=camera.transform.position;
        var rotation=camera.transform.rotation;
        var target=native.feet.position+Vector3.up*native.bodyHeight*.5f;
        try {
            foreach(float yaw in new[]{-20f,-24f,-28f}) {
                var inspection=Quaternion.Euler(19,yaw,0);
                camera.transform.SetPositionAndRotation(target+inspection*Quaternion.Inverse(rotation)*(pos-target),inspection);
                var plan=PlanWithCamera(native,camera,trials);
                if(plan!=null)return plan;
            }
        } finally {
            camera.transform.SetPositionAndRotation(pos,rotation);
        }
        return null;
    }

    static Vector2 CameraInput(Vector3 travel,Quaternion orient)=>new Vector2(
        Vector3.Dot(travel,PlayerMovement.CalculateCameraRelativeDirection(Vector2.right,orient)),
        Vector3.Dot(travel,PlayerMovement.CalculateCameraRelativeDirection(Vector2.up,orient)));

    public static WalkWorldStimulus154 PlanOpen(NativePlayer395 native,Camera camera) {
        var travel=Horizontal(native.avatar.forward);
        var input=CameraInput(travel,camera.transform.rotation);
        var ground=GameObject.Find("Ground");
        var controller=native.player.GetComponent<CharacterController>();
        if(!ground||!controller||!ClearGround(native.player.position,travel,native.player,controller,
             ground.GetComponent<Collider>(),OpenDistances[2]))
            throw new InvalidOperationException("154 previously validated open walk does not have safe ground now");
        return new WalkWorldStimulus154(null,native.player.position,travel,camera.transform.position,
            camera.transform.rotation,input,new int[OpenDistances.Length]);
    }

    static WalkWorldStimulus154 PlanWithCamera(NativePlayer395 native, Camera camera,List<object> trials) {
        var player=native.player;
        var controller=player.GetComponent<CharacterController>();
        var ground=GameObject.Find("Ground");
        var groundCollider=ground?ground.GetComponent<Collider>():null;
        var head=native.host.main.motion.animation.target.GetBoneTransform(HumanBodyBones.Head);
        var face=native.host.head.motion.surface.outputs[1];
        var vertices=face.sharedMesh.vertices;
        if(!controller||!groundCollider||!head||vertices.Length<=2520||!camera.orthographic)
            throw new InvalidOperationException("154 original walk geometry or camera unavailable");
        var nowFacing=Quaternion.Inverse(native.avatar.rotation);
        Vector3[] LocalVertices(MeshFilter filter)=>filter.sharedMesh.vertices
            .Select(v=>nowFacing*(filter.transform.TransformPoint(v)-native.feet.position)).ToArray();
        var facePoints=LocalVertices(face);
        var hair=native.host.head.motion.surface.outputs[2];
        var hairPoints=LocalVertices(hair);
        var skinIndices=face.sharedMesh.GetIndices(3);
        var hairIndices=Enumerable.Range(0,hair.sharedMesh.subMeshCount)
            .SelectMany(s=>hair.sharedMesh.GetIndices(s)).ToArray();
        var eyeIndices=face.sharedMesh.GetIndices(4).Concat(face.sharedMesh.GetIndices(1)).ToArray();
        var feetOffset=native.feet.position-player.position;
        var orient=camera.transform.rotation;
        var travel=Vector3.back;
        var facing=Quaternion.LookRotation(travel,Vector3.up);
        var ray=Quaternion.Inverse(facing)*-camera.transform.forward;
        var neutralEyes=VisibleEyeTriangles(facePoints,eyeIndices,skinIndices,hairPoints,hairIndices,ray);
        if(neutralEyes.Length<3) {
            trials.Add(new {yaw=orient.eulerAngles.y,visibleNeutralEyeTriangles=neutralEyes.Length});
            return null;
        }
        foreach(string name in new[]{"Test_MerchantNPC_Base","Test_QuestNPC_Base"}) {
            var obj=GameObject.Find(name);
            var box=obj?obj.GetComponent<BoxCollider>():null;
            var renderer=obj?obj.GetComponent<MeshRenderer>():null;
            var mesh=obj?obj.GetComponent<MeshFilter>():null;
            if(!box||!box.enabled||box.isTrigger||!renderer||!renderer.enabled||renderer.forceRenderingOff||
               !mesh||!mesh.sharedMesh||mesh.sharedMesh.vertexCount!=24||!renderer.sharedMaterial||
               renderer.sharedMaterial.renderQueue>2500||
               (camera.cullingMask&(1<<obj.layer))==0||obj.scene.path!="Assets/Scenes/GameMap1.unity")
                continue;
            foreach(float clearance in new[]{.78f,.86f,.94f,1.02f})
            foreach(float initialBehind in new[]{2.85f,3.05f,3.25f}){
                var start=new Vector3(box.bounds.center.x-clearance,player.position.y,box.bounds.max.z+initialBehind);
                var shift=start+travel*WorldDistances[1]-player.position;
                shift.y=0;
                // In orthographic projection this translation changes display
                // placement only. The rays through each world-space eye stay parallel.
                var eyes=WorldDistances.SelectMany(s=>neutralEyes.Select(eye=>
                    camera.WorldToViewportPoint(start+travel*s+feetOffset+facing*eye-shift))).ToArray();
                float x=(eyes.Min(v=>v.x)+eyes.Max(v=>v.x))*.5f;
                float y=(eyes.Min(v=>v.y)+eyes.Max(v=>v.y))*.5f;
                float verticalSpan=2*camera.orthographicSize;
                shift+=camera.transform.right*((x-.5f)*verticalSpan*camera.aspect)+
                       camera.transform.up*((y-.62f)*verticalSpan);
                bool clear=ClearGround(start,travel,player,controller,groundCollider,WorldDistances[2]);
                var hits=clear?WorldDistances.Select(s=>EyeHits(start+travel*s+feetOffset,neutralEyes,
                    facing,camera,shift,box)).ToArray():Array.Empty<int>();
                trials.Add(new {name,yaw=orient.eulerAngles.y,clearance,initialBehind,
                    visibleNeutralEyeTriangles=neutralEyes.Length,clear,hits});
                if(!clear||hits[0]!=0||hits[2]!=0||hits[1]<3)continue;
                var input=CameraInput(travel,orient);
                if(Vector3.Dot(PlayerMovement.CalculateCameraRelativeDirection(input,orient),travel)<.9999f)continue;
                return new WalkWorldStimulus154(box,start,travel,camera.transform.position+shift,orient,input,hits);
            }
        }
        return null;
    }

    static bool ClearGround(Vector3 start, Vector3 direction, Transform player,
                            CharacterController controller, Collider ground,float distance) {
        if(!ground)return false;
        var scale=player.lossyScale;
        float radius=controller.radius*Mathf.Max(Mathf.Abs(scale.x),Mathf.Abs(scale.z));
        float cap=Mathf.Max(0,controller.height*Mathf.Abs(scale.y)*.5f-radius);
        for(int step=0;step<=Mathf.CeilToInt(distance/.10f);step++) {
            var p=start+direction*Mathf.Min(step*.10f,distance);
            var down=new Ray(p+Vector3.up*.65f,Vector3.down);
            if(!ground.Raycast(down,out var groundHit,1.7f))return false;
            var centre=p+Vector3.up*(controller.center.y*scale.y+.025f);
            var overlaps=Physics.OverlapCapsule(centre-Vector3.up*cap,centre+Vector3.up*cap,radius,
                                               Physics.DefaultRaycastLayers,QueryTriggerInteraction.Ignore);
            if(overlaps.Any(c=>c&&c!=ground&&!c.transform.IsChildOf(player)))return false;
        }
        return true;
    }

    static int EyeHits(Vector3 feet, Vector3[] eyes, Quaternion facing, Camera camera, Vector3 shift, BoxCollider box) {
        int hits=0;
        foreach(var eye in eyes) {
            var p=feet+facing*eye;
            var v=camera.WorldToViewportPoint(p-shift);
            if(v.x<.12f||v.x>.88f||v.y<.12f||v.y>.90f||v.z<camera.nearClipPlane||v.z>camera.farClipPlane)
                return -1;
            var ray=camera.ViewportPointToRay(v);
            ray.origin+=shift;
            if(box.Raycast(ray,out _,Vector3.Dot(p-ray.origin,ray.direction)-.001f))hits++;
        }
        return hits;
    }

    static Vector3[] VisibleEyeTriangles(Vector3[] face, int[] eye, int[] skin, Vector3[] hair,
                                         int[] hairIndices, Vector3 towardCamera) {
        var points=new List<Vector3>();
        int stride=3*Mathf.Max(1,eye.Length/(3*96));
        for(int i=0;i<eye.Length;i+=stride) {
            var a=face[eye[i]];var b=face[eye[i+1]];var c=face[eye[i+2]];
            if(Vector3.Dot(Vector3.Cross(b-a,c-a),towardCamera)<=0)continue;
            var p=(a+b+c)/3;
            var origin=p+towardCamera*.0002f;
            if(HitTriangles(origin,towardCamera,face,skin)||HitTriangles(origin,towardCamera,hair,hairIndices))continue;
            points.Add(p);
        }
        return points.ToArray();
    }

    static bool HitTriangles(Vector3 origin,Vector3 ray,Vector3[] vertices,int[] index) {
        for(int i=0;i<index.Length;i+=3) {
            var a=vertices[index[i]];
            var ab=vertices[index[i+1]]-a;
            var ac=vertices[index[i+2]]-a;
            var normal=Vector3.Cross(ray,ac);
            float det=Vector3.Dot(ab,normal);
            if(Mathf.Abs(det)<1e-10f)continue;
            var from=origin-a;
            float u=Vector3.Dot(from,normal)/det;
            if(u<0||u>1)continue;
            var plane=Vector3.Cross(from,ab);
            float v=Vector3.Dot(ray,plane)/det;
            if(v<0||u+v>1)continue;
            float t=Vector3.Dot(ac,plane)/det;
            if(t>.0001f&&t<.5f)return true;
        }
        return false;
    }

    public object Describe() => new {
        blocker=Blocker?Blocker.name:null, blockerId=Blocker?Blocker.GetEntityId().ToString():null,
        boxMin=Blocker?XYZ(Blocker.bounds.min):null,boxMax=Blocker?XYZ(Blocker.bounds.max):null,
        start=XYZ(Start), camera=XYZ(CameraPosition), cameraEuler=XYZ(CameraRotation.eulerAngles), direction=XYZ(Direction), distances=Distances,
        eyeHitBudget=PlannedEyeHits
    };

    public WalkWorldStimulus154 WithCameraPosition(Vector3 position) =>
        new WalkWorldStimulus154(Blocker,Start,Direction,position,CameraRotation,Input,PlannedEyeHits);

    public float[] BoxEyeDepth(Camera camera,Matrix4x4 worldView,int x0,int y0,int width,int height) {
        var result=new float[width*height];
        for(int y=0;y<height;y++)for(int x=0;x<width;x++) {
            var ray=camera.ViewportPointToRay(new Vector3((x0+x+.5f)/960,(y0+y+.5f)/544,0));
            if(Blocker.Raycast(ray,out var hit,camera.farClipPlane))
                result[y*width+x]=-worldView.MultiplyPoint3x4(hit.point).z;
        }
        return result;
    }
}
}

namespace Unity.AI.Assistant.Agent.Dynamic.Extension.Editor {
// A bounded, validation-only style plan. The existing presentation still owns
// all geometry, coverage, occlusion, role, depth and screen address decisions.
public static class ContourPresentationTrial154 {
    public sealed class Edit {
        public int X, Y, Eye;
        public Vector2Int Cell;
        public Vector4 Colour;
        public float StyleDepth;
        public int ExactRays;
    }
    public sealed class Plan {
        public readonly List<Edit> Edits=new List<Edit>();
        public readonly HashSet<int> Protected=new HashSet<int>();
    }
    struct Tap {
        public Vector2Int Pixel,Cell;
        public Vector4 Layer;
        public float Weight;
        public int Address;
    }
    static Vector4 Read(float[] data,int i)=>new Vector4(data[i],data[i+1],data[i+2],data[i+3]);
    static bool Covered(Vector4 m)=>m.x>0&&m.y>.5f&&m.w>1e-6f;
    static bool EyeRole(float role)=>role==2||role==5||role==7;
    static float Ahead(Vector4 layer,float z)=>Covered(layer)&&layer.x<z?Mathf.Clamp01(layer.w):0;
    static float Visibility(int k,Vector4 s,Vector4 w,Vector4 i,Vector4 l) {
        if(k==3)return Mathf.Clamp01(l.w)*(1-Ahead(s,l.x))*(1-Ahead(w,l.x))*(1-Ahead(i,l.x));
        var own=k==1?w:i;var other=k==1?i:w;
        float a=Ahead(s,own.x),b=Ahead(other,own.x),c=Ahead(l,own.x);
        float result=Mathf.Clamp01(own.w)*(1-a)*(1-b)*(1-c);
        float front=Mathf.Max(a*(1-Ahead(other,s.x))*(1-Ahead(l,s.x)),
                     Mathf.Max(b*(1-Ahead(s,other.x))*(1-Ahead(l,other.x)),
                               c*(1-Ahead(s,l.x))*(1-Ahead(other,l.x))));
        return result>front?result:0;
    }
    public static Plan Build(Dictionary<string,float[]> rows,RectInt screen,RectInt roi,Vector2[] chart,
                             Vector3 mapping,Vector3 nearFar) {
        var result=new Plan();
        var display=new Dictionary<(int,Vector2Int),List<Vector2Int>>();
        int w=roi.width,h=roi.height,sw=screen.width;
        int nx=Mathf.FloorToInt(screen.x+mapping.x),ny=Mathf.FloorToInt(screen.y+mapping.y);
        int Address(Vector2Int p)=>4*(p.y*sw+p.x);
        bool Penetrates(Vector2Int local,float depth){
            var actual=local+screen.position;
            int ax=Mathf.FloorToInt(actual.x+.5f+mapping.x)-nx;
            int ay=Mathf.FloorToInt(actual.y+.5f+mapping.y)-ny;
            var head=ax>=0&&ax<=sw&&ay>=0&&ay<=screen.height?
                Read(rows["headNative"],4*(ay*(sw+1)+ax)):Vector4.zero;
            float device=rows["worldDevice"][Address(local)];
            float world=Mathf.Lerp(nearFar.x,nearFar.y,nearFar.z>.5f?1-device:device);
            if(depth>world+.0001f)return true;
            if(head.x>0&&head.w>0&&head.x+mapping.z<depth&&(head.y==6||head.y>=12))return true;
            var owner=Read(rows["owner"],Address(local));
            return owner.x>=8&&owner.x<=11&&owner.z>0&&owner.z<depth;
        }
        bool ExactLid(Vector2Int local,int eye){
            var p=local+screen.position;
            int sx=2*(Mathf.FloorToInt(p.x+.5f+mapping.x)-2*roi.x);
            int sy=2*(Mathf.FloorToInt(p.y+.5f+mapping.y)-2*roi.y);
            if(sx<0||sy<0||sx>=4*w||sy>=4*h)return false;
            int i=4*(sy*4*w+sx);
            var surface=Read(rows["physicalMeta3"],i);
            return Covered(surface)&&surface.z==eye&&!Penetrates(local,surface.x+mapping.z)&&
                Visibility(3,Read(rows["skinMeta"],i),Read(rows["physicalMeta1"],i),
                           Read(rows["physicalMeta2"],i),surface)>.5f;
        }
        for(int y=0;y<screen.height;y++)for(int x=0;x<sw;x++){
            var p=new Vector2Int(x,y);int i=Address(p);
            var owner=Read(rows["owner"],i);
            if(!EyeRole(owner.x)||(owner.y!=1&&owner.y!=2))continue;
            var address=Read(rows["address"],i);
            if(owner.y!=address.z||owner.z<=0||Penetrates(p,owner.z))
                throw new InvalidOperationException("154 mask/gather depth or address mismatch");
            result.Protected.Add(i);
            var key=((int)owner.y,new Vector2Int((int)address.x,(int)address.y));
            if(!display.TryGetValue(key,out var points))display.Add(key,points=new List<Vector2Int>());
            points.Add(p);
        }
        var layer0=rows["s0"];var layer1=rows["s1"];var layer2=rows["s2"];var layer3=rows["s3"];
        for(int lane=0;lane<2;lane++){
            int eye=2-lane;
            var origin=roi.position-Vector2Int.FloorToInt(chart[lane]);
            var layout=new Dictionary<Vector2Int,int>();
            for(int y=0;y<h;y++)for(int x=0;x<w;x++){
                var owner=Read(rows["liveEyeOwner"],4*(y*4*w+lane*w+x));
                if(EyeRole(owner.x)&&owner.y==eye)layout.Add(origin+new Vector2Int(x,y),(int)owner.x);
            }
            var roof=Enumerable.Repeat(-1,4*w).ToArray();
            var trace=new Dictionary<Vector2Int,Tap>();
            for(int sy=0;sy<4*h;sy++)for(int sx=0;sx<4*w;sx++){
                var cell=origin+new Vector2Int(sx/4,sy/4);
                if(!layout.ContainsKey(cell))continue;
                int i=4*(sy*16*w+lane*4*w+sx);
                var s=Read(layer0,i);var white=Read(layer1,i);var iris=Read(layer2,i);var lid=Read(layer3,i);
                if((Covered(white)&&white.z==eye&&rows["point1"][i+3]>.5f&&Visibility(1,s,white,iris,lid)>.5f)||
                   (Covered(iris)&&iris.z==eye&&rows["point2"][i+3]>.5f&&Visibility(2,s,white,iris,lid)>.5f))
                    roof[sx]=sy;
                if(!Covered(lid)||lid.z!=eye||rows["point3"][i+3]<=.5f)continue;
                float weight=Visibility(3,s,white,iris,lid);
                if(weight<=.5f)continue;
                var p=new Vector2Int(sx,sy);
                trace.Add(p,new Tap{Pixel=p,Cell=cell,Layer=lid,Weight=weight,Address=i});
            }
            var remaining=new HashSet<Vector2Int>(trace.Keys.Where(p=>roof[p.x]>=0&&p.y>=roof[p.x]-1));
            var connected=new List<Tap>();
            while(remaining.Count>0) {
                var p=remaining.First();remaining.Remove(p);
                var group=new List<Tap>();
                var queue=new Queue<Vector2Int>();queue.Enqueue(p);
                bool reachesVisibleLid=false;
                while(queue.Count>0){
                    p=queue.Dequeue();var tap=trace[p];group.Add(tap);
                    reachesVisibleLid|=layout[tap.Cell]==7&&display.ContainsKey((eye,tap.Cell));
                    for(int dy=-1;dy<=1;dy++)for(int dx=-1;dx<=1;dx++){
                        var adjacent=p+new Vector2Int(dx,dy);
                        if(remaining.Remove(adjacent))queue.Enqueue(adjacent);
                    }
                }
                if(reachesVisibleLid)connected.AddRange(group);
            }
            var eligible=connected.GroupBy(t=>t.Cell).OrderBy(g=>g.Key.y).ThenBy(g=>lane==0?g.Key.x:-g.Key.x);
            foreach(var group in eligible) {
                var cell=group.Key;
                if(layout[cell]!=2||!layout.TryGetValue(cell+Vector2Int.down,out int lower)||lower!=2||
                   group.Count()<4||!display.TryGetValue((eye,cell),out var points))continue;
                int exact=points.Count(p=>ExactLid(p,eye));
                if(exact<2)continue; // One sample can never occupy a whole iris cell.
                var tap=group.OrderByDescending(t=>t.Weight)
                    .ThenBy(t=>Mathf.Abs(t.Pixel.x%4-1.5f)+Mathf.Abs(t.Pixel.y%4-1.5f))
                    .ThenByDescending(t=>t.Pixel.y%4).ThenBy(t=>lane==0?t.Pixel.x%4:-(t.Pixel.x%4)).First();
                float depth=tap.Layer.x+mapping.z;
                if(points.Any(p=>Penetrates(p,depth)||rows["owner"][Address(p)]!=2))continue;
                var rgb=Read(rows["rgb3"],tap.Address)*rows["lineShade"][tap.Address+2];
                if(!float.IsFinite(rgb.x)||!float.IsFinite(rgb.y)||!float.IsFinite(rgb.z))
                    throw new InvalidOperationException("154 non-finite source lid colour");
                foreach(var p in points)result.Edits.Add(new Edit{X=p.x,Y=p.y,Eye=eye,Cell=cell,
                    Colour=new Vector4(rgb.x,rgb.y,rgb.z,rows["colour"][Address(p)+3]),
                    StyleDepth=depth,ExactRays=exact});
            }
        }
        if(result.Edits.Count>64||result.Edits.Select(e=>e.Y*sw+e.X).Distinct().Count()!=result.Edits.Count||
           result.Edits.Any(e=>!result.Protected.Contains(4*(e.Y*sw+e.X))))
            throw new InvalidOperationException("154 trial style write budget or protection mask mismatch");
        return result;
    }
}
}

namespace Unity.AI.Assistant.Agent.Dynamic.Extension.Editor {
// An opt-in authoring input on the evaluated SkinnedMeshRenderer. The source
// asset, skinning, materials, eye surfaces, highlights and blink remain owned
// by the existing rig and are restored before the trial resources are retired.
public sealed class LowerApertureProfileTrial154 {
    public const float MaximumHeadLift = .0013f;
    public const float AuthoredCloseFraction = .34f;
    readonly SkinnedMeshRenderer renderer;
    readonly UnityEngine.Mesh original;
    readonly int closeIndex, profileIndex, originalCount;
    readonly Dictionary<int,float> lifted;
    readonly int[][] arcs, corners;
    public UnityEngine.Mesh Preview { get; }
    public object Definition => new {
        maximumHeadLift=MaximumHeadLift,authoredCloseFraction=AuthoredCloseFraction,
        sourceBlendShape=original.GetBlendShapeName(closeIndex),
        lowerArc=arcs,pinnedCorners=corners,
        vertices=lifted.OrderBy(p=>p.Key).Select(p=>new {id=p.Key,headLift=p.Value}).ToArray()
    };

    public LowerApertureProfileTrial154(SkinnedMeshRenderer renderer,EyeFeature348 eye,int closeIndex) {
        this.renderer=renderer;this.closeIndex=closeIndex;
        original=renderer.sharedMesh;originalCount=original.blendShapeCount;
        if(eye.modelEyes.Length!=2||closeIndex<0||original.GetBlendShapeFrameCount(closeIndex)<1)
            throw new InvalidOperationException("154 lower profile requires the original eye ring and authored blink");
        int count=original.vertexCount,frame=original.GetBlendShapeFrameCount(closeIndex)-1;
        if(Mathf.Abs(original.GetBlendShapeFrameWeight(closeIndex,frame)-100)>.001f)
            throw new InvalidOperationException("154 authored blink endpoint is not 100");
        var closure=new Vector3[count];
        original.GetBlendShapeFrameVertices(closeIndex,frame,closure,new Vector3[count],new Vector3[count]);
        var vertices=original.vertices;
        var headFromMesh=eye.sourceToRestHead;
        var meshFromHead=headFromMesh.inverse;
        var rest=vertices.Select(v=>headFromMesh.MultiplyPoint3x4(v)).ToArray();
        var skin=new HashSet<int>(original.GetIndices(3));
        var nonSkin=new HashSet<int>(Enumerable.Range(0,original.subMeshCount)
            .Where(index=>index!=3).SelectMany(index=>original.GetIndices(index)));
        arcs=new int[2][];corners=new int[2][];lifted=new Dictionary<int,float>();
        var profile=new Vector3[count];
        for(int side=0;side<2;side++){
            var model=eye.modelEyes[side];
            var ring=model.closedBoundary;
            if(!model.boundaryClosed||ring==null||ring.Distinct().Count()<8)
                throw new InvalidOperationException("154 lower profile has no closed boundary");
            var u=rest.Select(p=>(double)Vector3.Dot(p-model.centre,model.axisX)).ToArray();
            var down=rest.Select(p=>(double)-Vector3.Dot(p-model.centre,model.axisY)).ToArray();
            var upper=LashRegion590.UpperArc(ring,u,down);
            var authoredCanthi=side==0?new[]{2520,2487}:new[]{1986,1996};
            if(authoredCanthi.Any(v=>!ring.Contains(v)))
                throw new InvalidOperationException("154 original canthus registration is missing");
            var pins=new HashSet<int>(authoredCanthi.Concat(upper));
            corners[side]=authoredCanthi;
            double left=u[upper[0]],right=u[upper[upper.Length-1]];
            if(!(right>left))throw new InvalidOperationException("154 invalid lower arc axis");
            arcs[side]=ring.Distinct().Where(v=>!pins.Contains(v)).ToArray();
            int active=0;
            foreach(int index in arcs[side]){
                if(!skin.Contains(index)||nonSkin.Contains(index))continue;
                float t=(float)((u[index]-left)/(right-left));
                float up=Vector3.Dot(headFromMesh.MultiplyVector(closure[index]),model.axisY);
                if(!(t>0&&t<1&&up>0))continue;
                float amount=Mathf.Min(MaximumHeadLift,up*AuthoredCloseFraction)*(4*t*(1-t));
                if(amount<=1e-6f)continue;
                if(lifted.ContainsKey(index))throw new InvalidOperationException("154 lower lid aliases the opposite ring");
                lifted.Add(index,amount);
                profile[index]=meshFromHead.MultiplyVector(model.axisY*amount);
                active++;
            }
            if(active<3)throw new InvalidOperationException("154 insufficient authored upward lower-lid support: eye "+model.stableId+" / "+active);
        }
        // Allocation happens only after source and rig checks pass. Add the
        // smallest local positional deformation to the transient preview mesh.
        Preview=UnityEngine.Object.Instantiate(original);
        Preview.name="154 owned optional lower aperture profile";
        Preview.hideFlags=HideFlags.HideAndDontSave;
        try {
            Preview.AddBlendShapeFrame("__154_validation_lower_aperture",100,profile,null,null);
            profileIndex=Preview.GetBlendShapeIndex("__154_validation_lower_aperture");
            if(profileIndex!=originalCount)
                throw new InvalidOperationException("154 profile did not append after original facial channels");
            Exchange(original,Preview);
        } catch {
            if(renderer.sharedMesh==Preview)Exchange(Preview,original);
            UnityEngine.Object.DestroyImmediate(Preview);
            throw;
        }
    }
    void Exchange(UnityEngine.Mesh from,UnityEngine.Mesh to) {
        if(renderer.sharedMesh!=from)
            throw new InvalidOperationException("154 unexpected shared face mesh during the scoped trial");
        var weights=Enumerable.Range(0,originalCount).Select(i=>renderer.GetBlendShapeWeight(i)).ToArray();
        renderer.sharedMesh=to;
        for(int i=0;i<weights.Length;i++)renderer.SetBlendShapeWeight(i,weights[i]);
    }
    public float Toggle(bool enabled) {
        if(renderer.sharedMesh!=Preview)throw new InvalidOperationException("154 trial profile detached");
        float covered=Mathf.Clamp01(renderer.GetBlendShapeWeight(closeIndex)/100);
        float weight=enabled?100*(1-covered):0;
        renderer.SetBlendShapeWeight(profileIndex,weight);
        return weight;
    }
    public float[] OriginalWeights()=>Enumerable.Range(0,originalCount).Select(i=>renderer.GetBlendShapeWeight(i)).ToArray();
    public void Restore()=>Exchange(Preview,original);
    public object Compare(Vector3[] off,Vector3[] on,Matrix4x4 localToWorld) {
        if(off.Length!=original.vertexCount||on.Length!=off.Length)
            throw new InvalidOperationException("154 baked topology changed during eye profile");
        var changed=new List<int>();
        float maxWorldLift=0;
        for(int i=0;i<off.Length;i++){
            if(off[i].Equals(on[i]))continue;
            if(!lifted.ContainsKey(i))throw new InvalidOperationException("154 profile moved non-target vertex "+i);
            changed.Add(i);
            maxWorldLift=Mathf.Max(maxWorldLift,localToWorld.MultiplyVector(on[i]-off[i]).magnitude);
        }
        if(corners.SelectMany(v=>v).Any(v=>!off[v].Equals(on[v])))
            throw new InvalidOperationException("154 profile moved an eye corner");
        return new {verticesCompared=off.Length,changed=changed.Count,changedIndices=changed,maxWorldLift,
                    cornerDifferences=0,outsideTargetDifferences=0};
    }
}
}

namespace Unity.AI.Assistant.Agent.Dynamic.Extension.Editor {
public static class T3ApertureTrial154 {
public static object Start(string cache,string run,string stage) {
var n=NativePlayer395.Active;
var build=Path.Combine(cache,"build");
var presentUse=new ApertureTrialUse154();
bool quarterOnly=stage=="quarter", regression=stage=="regression", frontalOnly=stage=="front", remaining=stage=="remaining";
 if(stage!="native-common-grid")throw new ArgumentException("154 opt-in R25 entry requires native-common-grid");
 bool commonGrid=true;
 var nativeCrop=new RectInt(384,0,192,352);
bool exitColour=stage=="exit-colour";
bool runtimeContour=stage=="contour-runtime";
bool lowerWalkOnly=stage=="lower-profile-walk";
bool lowerProfile=stage=="lower-profile"||lowerWalkOnly;
bool contourTransition=stage=="contour-transition"||runtimeContour||lowerProfile;
bool matchedSkin=exitColour||contourTransition||commonGrid;
bool worldCascadeFit=stage=="world-cascade"||exitColour;
bool worldOnly=stage=="world-only"||worldCascadeFit;
bool walkWorld=stage=="walk-world"||worldOnly;
if(!quarterOnly&&!regression&&!frontalOnly&&!remaining&&!walkWorld&&!contourTransition&&!commonGrid)throw new ArgumentException("154 unknown trial view set");
string renderFailure=null,coverageFailure=null;
bool unexpectedNativeError=false;
bool KnownShaderFailure(string message)=>new[]{"T2_DirectionalProbe154","T2_ActorBody154","T2_ActorToon154"}
  .Any(name=>message.StartsWith("Shader error in 'Hidden/EUnity/"+name+"':",StringComparison.Ordinal));
bool Owned(UnityEngine.Object obj)=>obj.name.StartsWith("154 owned ")||
 obj.name.StartsWith("__T3IntegratedAperture154")||
 obj.name=="Hidden/EUnity/T2_ActorBody154"||obj.name=="Hidden/EUnity/T2_ActorToon154"||
 obj.name=="Hidden/EUnity/T2_DirectionalProbe154"||obj.name=="Hidden/EUnity/T3ColourAudit348";
void LogRenderFailure(string message,string stack,LogType type){
 if(type!=LogType.Exception&&type!=LogType.Error)return;
 if(renderFailure==null)renderFailure=message;
 if(!KnownShaderFailure(message))unexpectedNativeError=true;
}
if(NativeWorldFeature395.IntegratedPresentation154!=null||NativeWorldFeature395.TryReleaseIntegrated154!=null||
    NativeWorldFeature395.QueueCompletion154!=null||NativeWorldFeature395.GraphOwner154!=null)
 throw new InvalidOperationException("154 another presentation owner is active");
if(!SystemInfo.supportsAsyncGPUReadback)
 throw new InvalidOperationException("154 asynchronous final-output receipt is unsupported");
if(Resources.FindObjectsOfTypeAll<UnityEngine.Object>().Any(Owned))
 throw new InvalidOperationException("154 previous trial resources are still retained");
var resultPath=Path.Combine(run,"FRAME_DATA.json");
if(File.Exists(resultPath)||File.Exists(resultPath+".started"))
 throw new InvalidOperationException("154 this run id has already started");

  Read(stockBase,"owners",n.owner.rt,sx,sy,sw,sh);
  Read(stockBase,"addresses",n.cellID.rt,sx,sy,sw,sh);
  var addresses=stockBase["addresses"];
  for(int k=0;k<addresses.Length;k+=4){
   if(stockBase["owners"][k+2]<=0)continue;
   int u=Mathf.RoundToInt(addresses[k]),v=Mathf.RoundToInt(addresses[k+1]);
   if(u<0||u>=480||v<0||v>=272)continue;
   u0=Math.Min(u0,u);u1=Math.Max(u1,u);v0=Math.Min(v0,v);v1=Math.Max(v1,v);
  }
  if(u0>u1||v0>v1)throw new InvalidOperationException("154 no visible character in stocking test region");
  Read(stockBase,"partAndRamp",canvas.fields[3].rt,u0,v0,u1-u0+1,v1-v0+1);
 }
 if(regression){
  cs.SetInt("_T3BypassEye154",1);
  presentUse.BeginPresent();
  Present();
  RecordStockBasis();
 }
 cs.SetInt("_T3BypassEye154",0);
 var headTrialRoot=n.avatar.position;
 var trialBasis=Matrix4x4.TRS(headTrialRoot,n.avatar.rotation,Vector3.one);
 var trialReference=e.pairedReferenceWorld348;
 if(lowerProfile){
  lowerTrial=new LowerApertureProfileTrial154(controls,e,close);

 }
 var pathMovement=n.player.GetComponent<PlayerMovement>();
 Vector2 pathInput=Vector2.zero;
 Vector3 pathDirection=Vector3.zero,normalFoot=Vector3.zero;
 Matrix4x4 normalReference=default;
 Vector3[] priorWalk=null;
 Vector3[] BakeCurrentReference(){
  if(!cpuFace)cpuFace=new UnityEngine.Mesh{name="154 CPU source face BakeMesh (never drawn)",hideFlags=HideFlags.HideAndDontSave};
  // Independently evaluated original skinning. Both lanes use the same
  // topology, authored rest attributes, and optionally the same profile.
  controls.BakeMesh(cpuFace,true);
  var vertices=cpuFace.vertices;
  if(vertices.Length!=frozen.vertexCount)throw new InvalidOperationException("154 independent skinning topology changed");
  var output=f.motion.surface.outputs[1].transform;
  if(matchedSkin){
   var map=output.worldToLocalMatrix*controls.localToWorldMatrix;
   var normal=map.inverse.transpose;
   frozen.vertices=vertices.Select(v=>map.MultiplyPoint3x4(v)).ToArray();
   frozen.normals=cpuFace.normals.Select(v=>normal.MultiplyVector(v).normalized).ToArray();
   frozen.tangents=cpuFace.tangents.Select(t=>{var v=map.MultiplyVector(t);return new Vector4(v.x,v.y,v.z,t.w);}).ToArray();
  }else{
   frozen.vertices=vertices;frozen.normals=cpuFace.normals;frozen.tangents=cpuFace.tangents;
  }
  frozen.RecalculateBounds();
  e.pairedYaw348=Quaternion.identity;
  e.pairedPivot348=n.avatar.position;
  e.pairedReferenceWorld348=matchedSkin?output.localToWorldMatrix:controls.localToWorldMatrix;
  return vertices;
 }
 object AdvanceAndBake(float distance){
  float Progress()=>Vector3.Dot(n.player.position-walk.Start,walk.Direction);
  if(!pathMovement||!pathMovement.isActiveAndEnabled||pathMovement.speed<=0)
   throw new InvalidOperationException("154 original walking movement inactive");
  float before=Progress(),elapsed=0;
  for(int i=0;i<360&&Progress()<distance-.0002f;i++){
   float delta=Mathf.Min(1f/60,(distance-Progress())/pathMovement.speed);
   pathMovement.StepMove(walk.Input,delta,false);
   animator.Update(delta);
   elapsed+=delta;
   if(Progress()<=before+.000001f)throw new InvalidOperationException("154 walk collided before its sampled frame");
   before=Progress();
  }
  if(Mathf.Abs(Progress()-distance)>.01f)
   throw new InvalidOperationException("154 valid walk sample could not reach the planned distance");
  var clips=animator.GetCurrentAnimatorClipInfo(0).Select(c=>new {name=c.clip.name,c.weight}).ToArray();
  if(!clips.Any(c=>c.name=="Rig|Walk_Loop"&&c.weight>.5f))
   throw new InvalidOperationException("154 active locomotion controller did not evaluate its walking clip");
  n.avatar.position=n.feet.position;
  var vertices=lowerProfile?Array.Empty<Vector3>():BakeCurrentReference();
  var rootFromSource=n.avatar.worldToLocalMatrix*controls.localToWorldMatrix;
  var rootLocal=vertices.Select(v=>rootFromSource.MultiplyPoint3x4(v)).ToArray();
  float changed=priorWalk==null||rootLocal.Length==0?0:rootLocal.Select((v,i)=>Vector3.Distance(v,priorWalk[i])).Max();
  priorWalk=rootLocal;
  var bones=new[]{HumanBodyBones.Head,HumanBodyBones.Hips,HumanBodyBones.LeftFoot,HumanBodyBones.RightFoot};
  return new {distance,actualDistance=Progress(),elapsed,clips,normalizedTime=animator.GetCurrentAnimatorStateInfo(0).normalizedTime,
    locomotion=animator.GetFloat(PlayerMovement.LocomotionSpeedParameter),maxRootLocalFaceDelta=changed,
    bones=bones.Select(b=>M(n.avatar.worldToLocalMatrix*animator.GetBoneTransform(b).localToWorldMatrix)).ToArray()};
 }
 void WriteCase(string name,object value){
  string file=Path.Combine(run,name+".json");
  using var stream=new FileStream(file,FileMode.CreateNew,FileAccess.Write);
  using var writer=new StreamWriter(stream);
  writer.Write(Newtonsoft.Json.JsonConvert.SerializeObject(value));
 }
 object TraceLid(Dictionary<string,float[]> rows){
  // One diagnostic address from R12. No shader, producer or output is modified.
  const int side=1,localX=1,localY=1;
  var roi=e.roi;
  int bottom=272-roi.yMax,tileWidth=roi.width*4;
  var mat=e.materials[10];
  var tex=mat.GetTexture("_MainTex") as Texture2D;
  object texDescription=tex?new {tex.name,tex.width,tex.height,tex.mipmapCount,tex.anisoLevel,tex.mipMapBias,
    filter=tex.filterMode.ToString(),wrapU=tex.wrapModeU.ToString(),wrapV=tex.wrapModeV.ToString(),
    format=tex.format.ToString(),sRGB=UnityEngine.Experimental.Rendering.GraphicsFormatUtility.IsSRGBFormat(tex.graphicsFormat),
    readable=tex.isReadable}:null;
  var tint=mat.GetColor("_Color");
  var lanes=new List<object>();
  for(int lane=0;lane<2;lane++){
   int tile=side+2*lane;
   var a=lane==0?e.pairedLocalAnchors348[side]:e.projectedReference348[side];
   int x=Mathf.FloorToInt(a.x)-roi.x+localX,y=Mathf.FloorToInt(a.y)-bottom+localY;
   if(x<0||x>=roi.width||y<0||y>=roi.height)throw new InvalidOperationException("154 target lid cell left the replay window");
   int cell=4*(y*roi.width*4+tile*roi.width+x);
   int ray=Mathf.RoundToInt(rows["liveEyeSelected"][cell+3]);
   if(ray<0||ray>=16){
    lanes.Add(new {lane,ray,owner=rows["liveEyeOwner"].Skip(cell).Take(4).ToArray()});
    continue;
   }
   int u=x*4+3-(ray&3)+tile*tileWidth,v=y*4+(ray>>2);
   var quad=new Dictionary<string,float[]>();
   Read(quad,"uvPrimitive",e.samples[3].uv.rt,u&~1,v&~1,2,2);
   int qi=4*(2*(v&1)+(u&1));
   var uv=quad["uvPrimitive"].Skip(qi).Take(4).ToArray();
   int primitive=Mathf.RoundToInt(uv[2])-1;
   var mesh=lane==0?e.sampleFace:frozen;
   var indices=mesh.GetIndices(6);
   var verts=mesh.vertices;var sourceUv=mesh.uv;
   var basis=lane==0?f.motion.surface.outputs[1].transform.localToWorldMatrix:e.pairedReferenceDraw348;
   var phase=lane==0?e.pairedPhase348[side]:a-new Vector2(Mathf.Floor(a.x)+.5f,Mathf.Floor(a.y)+.5f);
   var projection=e.cropProjection;
   projection.SetRow(0,projection.GetRow(0)-projection.GetRow(3)*(2*phase.x/roi.width));
   projection.SetRow(1,projection.GetRow(1)-projection.GetRow(3)*(2*phase.y/roi.height));
   var clip=projection*f.motion.camera.worldToCameraMatrix*basis;
   var triangle=primitive>=0&&primitive*3+2<indices.Length?indices.Skip(primitive*3).Take(3).Select(i=>{
    var c=clip*new Vector4(verts[i].x,verts[i].y,verts[i].z,1);
    return new {index=i,local=V(verts[i]),sourceUv=new[]{sourceUv[i].x,sourceUv[i].y},
      pixel=new[]{(c.x/c.w*.5f+.5f)*tileWidth+tile*tileWidth,(c.y/c.w*.5f+.5f)*roi.height*4},clipW=c.w};
   }).ToArray():null;
   var texels=new List<object>();
   if(tex&&tex.isReadable&&primitive>=0){
    try{
     for(int mip=0;mip<tex.mipmapCount;mip++){
      int tw=Mathf.Max(1,tex.width>>mip),th=Mathf.Max(1,tex.height>>mip);
      float tx=uv[0]*tw-.5f,ty=uv[1]*th-.5f;
      int ix=Mathf.FloorToInt(tx),iy=Mathf.FloorToInt(ty);
      int Address(int q,int size,TextureWrapMode wrap){
       if(wrap==TextureWrapMode.Repeat)return (q%size+size)%size;
       if(wrap==TextureWrapMode.Mirror){
        int at=(q%(size*2)+size*2)%(size*2);
        return at<size?at:size*2-1-at;
       }
       if(wrap==TextureWrapMode.MirrorOnce)q=q<0?-1-q:q;
       return Mathf.Clamp(q,0,size-1);
      }
      var samples=new List<float[]>();
      for(int dy=0;dy<2;dy++)for(int dx=0;dx<2;dx++){
       var c=tex.GetPixel(Address(ix+dx,tw,tex.wrapModeU),Address(iy+dy,th,tex.wrapModeV),mip);
       samples.Add(new[]{c.r,c.g,c.b,c.a});
      }
      texels.Add(new {mip,frac=new[]{tx-ix,ty-iy},samples});
     }
    }catch(UnityException error){texels.Add(new {unavailable=error.Message});}
   }
   int idx=4*(v*roi.width*16+u);
   lanes.Add(new {lane,ray,u,v,phase=new[]{phase.x,phase.y},uv,uvQuad=quad["uvPrimitive"],
     triangle,texels,owner=rows["liveEyeOwner"].Skip(cell).Take(4).ToArray(),
     src=rows["rgb3"].Skip(idx).Take(4).ToArray(),shade=rows["lineShade"].Skip(idx).Take(4).ToArray(),
     final=rows["liveEyeColour"].Skip(cell).Take(4).ToArray()});
  }
  var st=mat.GetTextureScale("_MainTex");var offset=mat.GetTextureOffset("_MainTex");
  return new {local=new[]{localX,localY},eye=side,mat.name,shader=mat.shader.name,texture=texDescription,
    tint=new[]{tint.r,tint.g,tint.b,tint.a},st=new[]{st.x,st.y,offset.x,offset.y},
    colourSpace=QualitySettings.activeColorSpace.ToString(),mipLimit=QualitySettings.globalTextureMipmapLimit,lanes};
 }
 void PrepareNormalPath(){
  if(!pathMovement||!pathMovement.isActiveAndEnabled||!float.IsFinite(pathMovement.speed)||pathMovement.speed<=0)
   throw new InvalidOperationException("154 original gameplay movement unavailable");
  if(!pathMovement.TryTeleport(pos0,playerRotation0,out string problem))
   throw new InvalidOperationException("154 cannot return to the normal gameplay start: "+problem);
  cam.transform.SetPositionAndRotation(camPos0,camRot0);
  n.avatar.rotation=actor0;
  animator.Play(state.fullPathHash,0,.5f);animator.Update(0);
  normalFoot=n.feet.position;
  normalReference=Matrix4x4.TRS(normalFoot,actor0,Vector3.one)*trialBasis.inverse*trialReference;
  var facing=Vector3.ProjectOnPlane(actor0*Vector3.forward,Vector3.up).normalized;
  pathInput=new Vector2(Vector3.Dot(facing,PlayerMovement.CalculateCameraRelativeDirection(Vector2.right,camRot0)),
     Vector3.Dot(facing,PlayerMovement.CalculateCameraRelativeDirection(Vector2.up,camRot0)));
  pathDirection=PlayerMovement.CalculateCameraRelativeDirection(pathInput,camRot0);
  if(Vector3.Dot(pathDirection,facing)<.9999f)throw new InvalidOperationException("154 normal movement direction changed facing");
  var route=new List<object>();
  foreach(float distance in new[]{0f,.25f,.5f}){
   var foot=normalFoot+distance*pathDirection;
   var groundHits=Physics.RaycastAll(foot+Vector3.up*.35f,Vector3.down,.85f,Physics.DefaultRaycastLayers,QueryTriggerInteraction.Ignore)
      .Where(hit=>hit.collider&&!hit.collider.transform.IsChildOf(n.player)).OrderBy(hit=>hit.distance).ToArray();
   var bottom=cam.WorldToViewportPoint(foot);
   var top=cam.WorldToViewportPoint(foot+Vector3.up*n.bodyHeight);
   bool fits=bottom.z>cam.nearClipPlane&&top.z>cam.nearClipPlane&&bottom.z<cam.farClipPlane&&top.z<cam.farClipPlane&&
      new[]{bottom,top}.All(v=>v.x>.08f&&v.x<.92f&&v.y>.05f&&v.y<.95f);
   route.Add(new {distance,foot=V(foot),bottom=V(bottom),top=V(top),fits,grounded=groundHits.Length>0});
   if(!fits||groundHits.Length==0){
    WriteCase("NORMAL_PATH_PREFLIGHT",new {camera=V(camPos0),rotation=Q(camRot0),direction=V(pathDirection),route,passed=false});
    throw new InvalidOperationException("154 full-length normal path fails ground/frustum preflight");
   }
  }
  WriteCase("NORMAL_PATH_PREFLIGHT",new {camera=V(camPos0),rotation=Q(camRot0),direction=V(pathDirection),route,passed=true});
  var root=cam.WorldToViewportPoint(normalFoot);
  var headView=cam.WorldToViewportPoint(normalFoot+Vector3.up*n.bodyHeight);
  sx=Mathf.Max(0,Mathf.FloorToInt(Mathf.Min(root.x,headView.x)*960)-86);
  sy=Mathf.Max(0,Mathf.FloorToInt(Mathf.Min(root.y,headView.y)*544)-18);
  sw=Mathf.Min(960-sx,174);
  sh=Mathf.Min(544-sy,Mathf.CeilToInt(Mathf.Max(root.y,headView.y)*544)+18-sy);
 }
 var entries=commonGrid?new[]{new[]{0f,0f,0f,0f},new[]{0f,0f,1f,0f}}:quarterOnly?new[]{new[]{0f,0f,1f,0f}}:
   frontalOnly?new[]{new[]{0f,0f,0f,0f}}:
   walkWorld?walk.Distances.Select(d=>new[]{0f,0f,0f,d}).ToArray():
   lowerWalkOnly?new[]{new[]{0f,0f,.42f,.14f},new[]{0f,0f,.42f,.18f},new[]{0f,0f,.42f,.22f}}
                     .SelectMany(pose=>new[]{pose,pose}).ToArray():
   lowerProfile?new[]{new[]{0f,0f,0f,0f},new[]{0f,0f,1f,0f},new[]{0f,50f,0f,0f},
                     new[]{0f,100f,0f,0f},new[]{0f,0f,.42f,.14f},
                     new[]{0f,0f,.42f,.18f},new[]{0f,0f,.42f,.22f}}
                     .SelectMany(pose=>new[]{pose,pose}).ToArray():
   runtimeContour?new[]{new[]{0f,0f,0f,0f},new[]{0f,0f,.24f,0f},new[]{0f,0f,.32f,0f},
                  new[]{0f,0f,.40f,0f},new[]{0f,0f,.68f,0f},new[]{0f,0f,1f,0f},
                  new[]{0f,0f,.42f,.14f},new[]{0f,0f,.42f,.18f},
                  new[]{0f,0f,.42f,.22f},new[]{0f,0f,.42f,.26f}}:

   remaining?new[]{new[]{-10f,0f,0f,0f},new[]{10f,0f,0f,0f},new[]{0f,100f,0f,0f},

         new[]{0f,100f,0f,0f},new[]{0f,0f,0f,0f},new[]{0f,0f,0f,1f}};
 foreach(var p in entries){
  lowerOn=lowerProfile&&poseIndex%2==1;
  if(!lowerOn){
   e.pairedYaw348=Quaternion.AngleAxis(p[0],axis);
   n.avatar.rotation=e.pairedYaw348*originalFacing;
   controls.SetBlendShapeWeight(close,p[1]);

  if(commonGrid)SetCamera(Quaternion.Slerp(headFront,quarterRotation,p[2]));

  Vector3 moveDelta=Vector3.zero;
  int phase=(int)p[3];
  lowerCase=lowerWalkOnly?new[]{"walk-14","walk-18","walk-22"}[poseIndex/2]:
     lowerProfile?new[]{"front","quarter","half","closed","walk-14","walk-18","walk-22"}[poseIndex/2]:null;

  object walkPose=null;
  if(lowerOn&&p[3]>0){

  }else if(contourTransition&&p[3]>0){

   // camera-relative movement before rotating the observation camera.

     throw new InvalidOperationException("154 original walk start unavailable: "+reason);

   }

   var right=forwardHead.right;
   if(Vector3.Dot(right,n.avatar.right)>0)right=-right;
   var frontal=Quaternion.LookRotation(Vector3.Cross(right,forwardHead.up),forwardHead.up);
   var oblique=Quaternion.AngleAxis(-10,Vector3.up)*frontal*Quaternion.Euler(30,0,0);
   var orient=Quaternion.Slerp(frontal,oblique,p[2]);
   var center=n.player.position+Vector3.up*.91f;
   cam.transform.SetPositionAndRotation(center-orient*Vector3.forward*viewDistance,orient);
  }else if(walkWorld){
   walkPose=AdvanceAndBake(p[3]);

  }else if(remaining&&phase>0){
   if(phase==1)PrepareNormalPath();
   n.avatar.rotation=actor0;
   e.pairedYaw348=Quaternion.identity;
   if(phase>=3){

     animator.Update(stepTime);
    }
   }
   moveDelta=n.player.position-pos0;
   if(phase>=3){
    float progress=Vector3.Dot(moveDelta,pathDirection);
    float requested=(phase-2)*.25f;
    if(Mathf.Abs(progress-requested)>.06f)
     throw new InvalidOperationException("154 actual movement did not achieve the planned travel: "+progress);
   }

   // real translation. Moving poses still use the live eye/skin witnesses.

   cs.SetInt("_T3BypassEye154",phase==1?1:0);

    throw new InvalidOperationException("154 movement input unavailable");
   var before=n.player.position;
   for(int i=0;i<4;i++)movement.StepMove(Vector2.right,.125f/movement.speed,false);
   moveDelta=n.player.position-before;
   if(new Vector2(moveDelta.x,moveDelta.z).magnitude<.35f)
    throw new InvalidOperationException("154 movement stimulus was blocked before presentation");
  }
  if(lowerProfile){
   lowerWeight=lowerTrial.Toggle(lowerOn);
   // Keep reference and live draw at the identical evaluated profile and pose.
   BakeCurrentReference();
  }
  if(commonGrid){
   BakeCurrentReference();Array.Clear(receiptData,0,receiptData.Length);
    receipts.SetData(receiptData);phaseReceipts=0;
    activeNative=new NativeOwnerGraph154(n,e,nativeCrop,nativeWorldProof,nativeOwners);
    NativeWorldFeature395.GraphOwner154=activeNative;
  }
  pendingStimulus=new {poseIndex,p,moveDelta=V(moveDelta),playerPosition=V(n.player.position),
    caseName,actorRotation=Q(n.avatar.rotation),cameraPosition=V(cam.transform.position),cameraRotation=Q(cam.transform.rotation),walkPose};
  if(remaining||walkWorld||contourTransition)WriteCase(caseName+"_INPUT",pendingStimulus);
  // Walk the same three animation segments. Only the disputed exit is presented.
  if(exitColour&&poseIndex<2){poseIndex++;continue;}

  cs.SetInt("_DebugOldEyeMarker348",!lowerProfile&&poseIndex==9?1:0);
  int bindsBefore=bindCount;int countBefore=canvasRecords;
  presentUse.BeginPresent();
   try{Present();}
   finally{NativeWorldFeature395.GraphOwner154=null;}
   if(commonGrid&&phaseReceipts!=1)throw new InvalidOperationException("154 expected one common-grid Final Present");
  if(bindCount==bindsBefore||canvasRecords!=countBefore+1)throw new InvalidOperationException("154 wrong producer frame/count");
 if(remaining||walkWorld||contourTransition)WriteCase(caseName+"_COVERAGE",new {split.Cascade,split.LastCoverage});
  if(remaining&&phase==1)RecordStockBasis();
  int cx=Mathf.RoundToInt((positions[0].x+positions[1].x)*.5f)-52;
  int cy=Mathf.RoundToInt((positions[0].y+positions[1].y)*.5f)-45;
  const int width=104,height=100;

  Read(rows,"colour",n.output.rt,cx,cy,width,height);
  // This reads the output of the last pass using the T3 resources. Later
  // diagnostic or file failures do not invalidate its completion proof.
   presentUse.Confirm();
   var firstOwner=new Unity.Mathematics.uint4[1];
   nativeOwners.GetData(firstOwner,0,0,1);
